    if (p.destinatario !== audiencia && p.destinatario !== 'ambos') return false;
    if (categoriaAtiva !== 'Tudo') {
      const cat = isEn && p.categoria_en ? p.categoria_en : p.categoria;
      if (cat !== categoriaAtiva) return false;
    }
    return true;
  });

  return (
    <main className="min-h-screen bg-slate-50 font-sans text-slate-900 pb-20">

      {/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════ */}
      {/* HEADER EDITORIAL                            */}
      {/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════ */}
      <section className="bg-slate-900 text-white pt-24 pb-32 text-center relative overflow-hidden">
        <div className="absolute inset-0 z-0 bg-[radial-gradient(ellipse_at_top,_var(--tw-gradient-stops))] from-slate-800 via-slate-900 to-black pointer-events-none" />
        
        <div className="max-w-4xl mx-auto px-4 relative z-10">
          <span className="inline-block text-[10px] font-black tracking-widest text-[#e2a41d] bg-amber-500/10 border border-amber-500/20 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-full uppercase mb-6">
            {isEn ? 'Editorial & Guides' : 'Editorial & Guias'}
          </span>
          <h1 className="text-4xl md:text-6xl font-black tracking-tight mb-6 leading-tight">
            {isEn ? 'HelloCamp ' : 'Recursos '}
            <span className="text-transparent bg-clip-text bg-gradient-to-r from-blue-400 to-emerald-400">
              {isEn ? 'Journal' : 'HelloCamp'}
            </span>
          </h1>
          <p className="text-slate-400 text-lg md:text-xl font-medium max-w-2xl mx-auto leading-relaxed">
            {isEn ? 'Expert articles, tips, and guides to help you plan the perfect summer experience.' : 'Artigos, dicas de especialistas e guias para o ajudar a planear a experiência de verão perfeita.'}
          </p>
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════ */}
      {/* NAVEGAÇÃO E FILTROS                         */}
      {/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════ */}
      <section className="max-w-6xl mx-auto px-4 md:px-8 py-10 -mt-16 relative z-20">

        {/* TOGGLE AUDIÊNCIA */}
        <div className="flex justify-center mb-10">
          <div className="flex bg-white p-1.5 rounded-2xl shadow-xl shadow-black/5 border border-slate-200">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setAudiencia('pais')}
              className={`px-8 py-3.5 rounded-xl text-[11px] font-black tracking-widest uppercase transition-all ${audiencia === 'pais' ? 'bg-[#0E3A68] text-white shadow-md' : 'text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-50'}`}
            >
              {isEn ? 'For Families' : 'Para Famílias'}
            </button>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setAudiencia('parceiros')}
              className={`px-8 py-3.5 rounded-xl text-[11px] font-black tracking-widest uppercase transition-all ${audiencia === 'parceiros' ? 'bg-[#167524] text-white shadow-md' : 'text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-50'}`}
            >
              {isEn ? 'For Organizers' : 'Para Organizadores'}
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* CATEGORIAS */}
        {!loading && categorias.length > 1 && (
          <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-3 mb-12 justify-center">
            {categorias.map(cat => (
              <button
                type="button"
                key={cat}
                onClick={() => setCategoriaAtiva(cat)}
                className={`px-5 py-2.5 rounded-full text-[11px] font-black tracking-wider uppercase transition-all border shadow-sm ${
                  categoriaAtiva === cat
                    ? (audiencia === 'pais' ? 'bg-[#0E3A68] border-[#0E3A68] text-white' : 'bg-[#167524] border-[#167524] text-white')
                    : 'bg-white border-slate-200 text-slate-500 hover:border-slate-300 hover:text-slate-700'
                }`}
              >
                {cat === 'Tudo' ? (isEn ? 'All Articles' : 'Todos os Artigos') : cat}
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
        )}

        {/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════ */}
        {/* GRELHA DE ARTIGOS                           */}
        {/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════ */}
        {loading ? (
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-8">
            {[1, 2, 3].map(i => (
              <div key={i} className="bg-white rounded-[2rem] border border-slate-200 overflow-hidden animate-pulse">
                <div className="h-60 bg-slate-200" />
                <div className="p-8 space-y-4">
                  <div className="h-4 bg-slate-200 rounded w-1/3" />
                  <div className="h-8 bg-slate-200 rounded w-full" />
                  <div className="h-4 bg-slate-200 rounded w-2/3" />
                </div>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        ) : postsFiltrados.length === 0 ? (
          <div className="text-center py-24 bg-white rounded-[2rem] border border-slate-200 shadow-sm max-w-2xl mx-auto">
            <div className="w-20 h-20 mx-auto mb-6 rounded-full bg-slate-50 border border-slate-100 flex items-center justify-center text-slate-300">
              <IconNews className="w-10 h-10" />
            </div>
            <h3 className="text-2xl font-black text-slate-900 mb-2">
              {isEn ? 'No articles yet' : 'Sem artigos publicados'}
            </h3>
            <p className="text-slate-500 font-medium">
              {isEn ? 'We are working on new content for this section.' : 'A nossa equipa editorial está a preparar conteúdo para esta secção.'}
            </p>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-8">
            {postsFiltrados.map((post) => {
              const titulo = isEn && post.titulo_en ? post.titulo_en : post.titulo;
              const resumo = isEn && post.resumo_en ? post.resumo_en : post.resumo;
              const categoria = isEn && post.categoria_en ? post.categoria_en : post.categoria;
              const isPartner = audiencia === 'parceiros';

              return (
                <Link
                  key={post.id}
                  href={`/${lang}/blog/${post.slug}`}
                  className="group bg-white rounded-[2rem] border border-slate-200 shadow-sm hover:shadow-[0_20px_60px_rgb(0,0,0,0.06)] transition-all duration-300 overflow-hidden flex flex-col hover:-translate-y-1"
                >
                  <div className="relative w-full h-60 bg-slate-100 overflow-hidden">
                    <img
                      src={post.imagem_url || FALLBACK_BLOG}
                      onError={(e) => { e.currentTarget.src = FALLBACK_BLOG; }}
                      alt={titulo}
                      loading="lazy"
                      className="w-full h-full object-cover transition-transform duration-700 group-hover:scale-105"
                    />
                    {categoria && (
                      <div className={`absolute top-4 left-4 backdrop-blur-md px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest shadow-sm ${isPartner ? 'bg-slate-900/90 text-white' : 'bg-white/95 text-[#0E3A68]'}`}>
                        {categoria}
                      </div>
                    )}
                  </div>
                  <div className="p-8 flex flex-col flex-1">
                    <p className="text-[10px] font-black text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-widest mb-3">
                      {formatarData(post.created_at)}
                    </p>
                    <h3 className={`text-xl font-black text-slate-900 leading-tight mb-3 transition-colors line-clamp-3 ${isPartner ? 'group-hover:text-[#167524]' : 'group-hover:text-[#0E3A68]'}`}>
                      {titulo}
                    </h3>
                    <p className="text-slate-500 text-sm font-medium leading-relaxed line-clamp-3 mb-8">
                      {resumo}
                    </p>
                    <div className={`mt-auto pt-5 border-t border-slate-100 flex items-center justify-between font-black text-[10px] uppercase tracking-widest ${isPartner ? 'text-[#167524]' : 'text-[#0E3A68]'}`}>
                      <span>{isEn ? 'Read Full Article' : 'Ler Artigo Completo'}</span>
                      <span className="bg-slate-50 p-2 rounded-full group-hover:bg-slate-100 transition-colors">
                        <IconArrowRight />
                      </span>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </Link>
              );
            })}
          </div>
        )}
      </section>
    </main>
  );
}
</file>

<file path="app/[lang]/(public)/cliente/avaliar/[reservaId]/page.tsx">
"use client";

import { useEffect, useState, use } from "react";
import { supabase } from "@/lib/supabase";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import Link from "next/link";
import React from "react";

export default function PaginaAvaliacao({ params }: { params: Promise<{ lang: string; reservaId: string }> }) {
  const { lang, reservaId } = use(params);
  const isEn = lang === 'en';
  const router = useRouter();

  const [reserva, setReserva] = useState<any>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [erro, setErro] = useState("");
  
  const [rating, setRating] = useState(0);
  const [hoverRating, setHoverRating] = useState(0);
  const [comentario, setComentario] = useState("");
  const [submetendo, setSubmetendo] = useState(false);
  const [sucesso, setSucesso] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    const fetchReserva = async () => {
      const { data: { session } } = await supabase.auth.getSession();
      if (!session) {
        router.push(`/${lang}/login`);
        return;
      }

      // Verifica se a reserva existe e pertence a este pai
      const { data, error } = await supabase
        .from("reservas")
        .select(`id, campo_id, criancas(nome), campos(nome, nome_en)`)
        .eq("id", reservaId)
        .eq("cliente_id", session.user.id)
        .single();

      if (error || !data) {
        setErro(isEn ? "Reservation not found." : "Reserva não encontrada.");
        setLoading(false);
        return;
      }

      // Verifica se já avaliou
      const { data: reviewExistente } = await supabase
        .from("reviews")
        .select("id")
        .eq("campo_id", data.campo_id)
        .eq("user_id", session.user.id)
        .single();

      if (reviewExistente) {
        setSucesso(true); 
      } else {
        setReserva(data);
      }
      
      setLoading(false);
    };
    fetchReserva();
  }, [reservaId, lang, router]);

  const submeterAvaliacao = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (rating === 0) return;
    setSubmetendo(true);

    const { data: { session } } = await supabase.auth.getSession();
    if (!session || !reserva) return;

    // 1. Inserir a Review
    await supabase.from("reviews").insert([{
      campo_id: reserva.campo_id,
      user_id: session.user.id,
      rating: rating,
      comentario: comentario,
      nome_pai: session.user.user_metadata?.full_name || "Anónimo"
    }]);

    // 2. Recalcular a Média do Campo
    const { data: todasReviews } = await supabase.from("reviews").select("rating").eq("campo_id", reserva.campo_id);
    if (todasReviews) {
      const total = todasReviews.length;
      const soma = todasReviews.reduce((acc, curr) => acc + curr.rating, 0);
      const media = soma / total;

      await supabase.from("campos").update({
        rating_score: media,
        total_reviews: total
      }).eq("id", reserva.campo_id);
    }

    setSucesso(true);
    setSubmetendo(false);
  };

  if (loading) return <div className="min-h-screen bg-slate-50 flex items-center justify-center font-bold text-slate-400 animate-pulse">{isEn ? 'Loading...' : 'A verificar dados...'}</div>;
  if (erro) return <div className="min-h-screen bg-slate-50 flex items-center justify-center font-black text-red-500">{erro}</div>;

  if (sucesso) {
    return (
      <main className="min-h-screen bg-slate-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 font-sans">
        <div className="bg-white p-10 md:p-14 rounded-[2rem] shadow-xl shadow-slate-200 border border-slate-100 text-center max-w-md w-full animate-in zoom-in-95 duration-500">
          <div className="w-24 h-24 bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-500 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center text-5xl mx-auto mb-8 shadow-inner border border-emerald-100">
            ✓
          </div>
          <h1 className="text-3xl font-black text-slate-900 mb-3 tracking-tight">{isEn ? 'Thank you!' : 'Muito Obrigado!'}</h1>
          <p className="text-slate-500 mb-10 font-medium leading-relaxed">{isEn ? 'Your review helps other parents make the best decision for their children.' : 'A sua avaliação foi submetida e vai ajudar outros pais a tomar a melhor decisão no próximo verão.'}</p>
          <Link href={`/${lang}/cliente/dashboard`} className="px-8 py-4 bg-[#0E3A68] text-white text-[11px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest rounded-xl block w-full no-underline hover:bg-[#0b2b4d] transition-all shadow-md hover:-translate-y-1">
            {isEn ? 'Back to Dashboard' : 'Voltar ao Meu Dashboard'}
          </Link>
        </div>
      </main>
    );
  }

  const nomeCampo = isEn && reserva.campos.nome_en ? reserva.campos.nome_en : reserva.campos.nome;

  return (
    <main className="min-h-screen bg-slate-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 font-sans">
      <div className="bg-white p-8 md:p-12 rounded-[2rem] shadow-2xl shadow-slate-200/50 border border-slate-100 max-w-xl w-full animate-in fade-in slide-in-from-bottom-4 duration-500">
        
        <div className="text-center mb-10">
          <span className="text-[10px] font-black text-[#e2a41d] uppercase tracking-widest bg-[#e2a41d]/10 px-4 py-1.5 rounded-full">{isEn ? 'Rate your experience' : 'Avalie a experiência'}</span>
          <h1 className="text-2xl md:text-3xl font-black text-slate-900 mt-6 mb-2 tracking-tight">{nomeCampo}</h1>
          <p className="text-slate-500 font-medium text-sm">{isEn ? 'Participant:' : 'Participante:'} <span className="text-slate-900 font-black">{reserva.criancas.nome}</span></p>
        </div>

        <form onSubmit={submeterAvaliacao} className="flex flex-col gap-10">
          
          {/* SISTEMA DE ESTRELAS INTERATIVO */}
          <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-4 bg-slate-50 p-6 rounded-2xl border border-slate-100">
            <label className="text-xs font-black text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-widest">{isEn ? 'Overall Rating' : 'Classificação Geral'}</label>
            <div className="flex gap-2 md:gap-4">
              {[1, 2, 3, 4, 5].map((star) => (
                <button 
                  key={star} 
                  type="button" 
                  onClick={() => setRating(star)}
                  onMouseEnter={() => setHoverRating(star)}
                  onMouseLeave={() => setHoverRating(0)}
                  className="text-5xl md:text-6xl transition-transform hover:scale-110 focus:outline-none leading-none"
                  style={{ color: star <= (hoverRating || rating) ? '#e2a41d' : '#e2e8f0', textShadow: star <= (hoverRating || rating) ? '0 4px 10px rgba(226, 164, 29, 0.3)' : 'none' }}
                >
                  ★
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>
            {rating === 0 && <p className="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-widest text-[#e2a41d] mt-2 animate-pulse">{isEn ? 'Select a rating to continue' : 'Clique nas estrelas para pontuar'}</p>}
          </div>

          <div className="flex flex-col gap-3">
            <label className="text-[10px] font-black text-slate-500 uppercase tracking-widest ml-1">{isEn ? 'Write a review (Optional)' : 'Escreva um comentário (Opcional)'}</label>
            <textarea 
              value={comentario} 
              onChange={e => setComentario(e.target.value)} 
              placeholder={isEn ? "How was the experience? What did your child like the most?" : "Como correu a experiência? A equipa de monitores foi simpática? O que o seu filho gostou mais?"}
              className="w-full p-5 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 text-sm font-medium text-slate-900 outline-none focus:border-[#0E3A68] focus:bg-white resize-none h-36 transition-colors shadow-inner"
            ></textarea>
          </div>

          <button 
            type="submit" 
            disabled={rating === 0 || submetendo} 
            className={`w-full py-4 rounded-xl text-sm uppercase tracking-widest font-black transition-all ${rating === 0 || submetendo ? 'bg-slate-100 text-slate-400 border border-slate-200 cursor-not-allowed' : 'bg-[#0E3A68] text-white shadow-xl shadow-[#0E3A68]/20 hover:bg-[#0b2b4d] hover:-translate-y-1 cursor-pointer'}`}
          >
            {submetendo ? (isEn ? 'Submitting...' : 'A enviar avaliação...') : (isEn ? 'Submit Review' : 'Enviar Avaliação')}
          </button>

        </form>
      </div>
    </main>
  );
}
</file>

<file path="app/[lang]/(public)/cliente/criancas/[id]/page.tsx">
"use client";

import { useEffect, useState, use } from "react";
import { supabase } from "@/lib/supabase";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import Link from "next/link";
import React from "react";

const selectClass = "w-full p-4 bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl text-sm font-bold text-slate-800 outline-none focus:border-[#0E3A68] focus:bg-white appearance-none cursor-pointer transition-colors shadow-sm bg-[url('data:image/svg+xml;charset=US-ASCII,%3Csvg%20xmlns%3D%22http%3A%2F%2Fwww.w3.org%2F2000%2Fsvg%22%20width%3D%22292.4%22%20height%3D%22292.4%22%3E%3Cpath%20fill%3D%22%2364748b%22%20d%3D%22M287%2069.4a17.6%2017.6%200%200%200-13-5.4H18.4c-5%200-9.3%201.8-12.9%205.4A17.6%2017.6%200%200%200%200%2082.2c0%205%201.8%209.3%205.4%2012.9l128%20127.9c3.6%203.6%207.8%205.4%2012.8%205.4s9.2-1.8%2012.8-5.4L287%2095c3.5-3.5%205.4-7.8%205.4-12.8%200-5-1.9-9.2-5.5-12.8z%22%2F%3E%3C%2Fsvg%3E')] bg-[length:0.8rem_auto] bg-[position:right_1rem_center] bg-no-repeat";
const inputClass = "w-full p-4 bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl text-sm font-bold text-slate-800 outline-none focus:border-[#0E3A68] focus:bg-white transition-colors shadow-sm placeholder-slate-400";
const labelClass = "block text-[11px] font-black text-slate-500 uppercase tracking-widest mb-2";

export default function EditarCrianca({ params }: { params: Promise<{ lang: string; id: string }> }) {
  const { lang, id } = use(params);
  const isEn = lang === 'en';
  const router = useRouter();

  const isModoCriacao = id === 'novo';

  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  
  const [formData, setFormData] = useState({ 
    nome: '', nif: '', data_nascimento: '', sexo: '', tipo_sanguineo: '',
    restricoes_alimentares: '', doencas_cronicas: '', medicacao_regular: '', limitacoes_fisicas: '',
    sabe_nadar: '', sabe_andar_bicicleta: '', tamanho_tshirt: '',
    autorizacao_fotografia: '', nivel_ingles: '', fobias_medos: '', perfil_comportamental: ''
  });

  useEffect(() => {
    const fetchCrianca = async () => {
      if (isModoCriacao) {
        setLoading(false);
        return;
      }

      const { data } = await supabase.from('criancas').select('*').eq('id', id).single();
      if (data) {
        setFormData({ 
          nome: data.nome || '', nif: data.nif || '', data_nascimento: data.data_nascimento || '',
          sexo: data.sexo || '', tipo_sanguineo: data.tipo_sanguineo || '',
          restricoes_alimentares: data.restricoes_alimentares || '', doencas_cronicas: data.doencas_cronicas || '',
          medicacao_regular: data.medicacao_regular || '', limitacoes_fisicas: data.limitacoes_fisicas || '',
          sabe_nadar: data.sabe_nadar || '', sabe_andar_bicicleta: data.sabe_andar_bicicleta || '',
          tamanho_tshirt: data.tamanho_tshirt || '', autorizacao_fotografia: data.autorizacao_fotografia || '',
          nivel_ingles: data.nivel_ingles || '', fobias_medos: data.fobias_medos || '',
          perfil_comportamental: data.perfil_comportamental || ''
        });
      }
      setLoading(false);
    };
    fetchCrianca();
  }, [id, isModoCriacao]);

  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setSaving(true);
    
    try {
      const { data: { session } } = await supabase.auth.getSession();
      if (!session) throw new Error("Sessão expirada.");

      if (isModoCriacao) {
        const { error } = await supabase.from('criancas').insert({ cliente_id: session.user.id, ...formData });
        if (error) throw error;
      } else {
        const { error } = await supabase.from('criancas').update(formData).eq('id', id);
        if (error) throw error;
      }

      alert(isEn ? 'Profile saved successfully!' : 'Perfil guardado com sucesso!');
      router.push(`/${lang}/cliente/criancas`);
    } catch (err: any) { alert('Erro ao guardar: ' + err.message); } 
    finally { setSaving(false); }
  };

  const handleApagar = async () => {
    if (!window.confirm(isEn ? 'Are you sure you want to delete this profile?' : 'Tem a certeza que deseja apagar o perfil do seu filho(a)? O histórico poderá ser perdido.')) return;
    await supabase.from('criancas').delete().eq('id', id);
    router.push(`/${lang}/cliente/criancas`);
  };

  if (loading) return <div className="p-20 text-center font-bold text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-widest text-xs animate-pulse">{isEn ? 'Loading profile...' : 'A preparar ficha...'}</div>;

  return (
    <div className="max-w-[850px] mx-auto pb-24 font-sans">
      
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row justify-between items-start sm:items-center gap-4 mb-8">
        <Link href={`/${lang}/cliente/criancas`} className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-900 font-bold text-sm transition-colors bg-white px-5 py-2.5 rounded-full border border-slate-200 shadow-sm no-underline">
          &larr; {isEn ? 'Back to list' : 'Voltar à lista'}
        </Link>
        
        {!isModoCriacao && (
          <button type="button" onClick={handleApagar} className="text-red-500 hover:bg-red-50 border border-red-200 hover:border-red-300 px-5 py-2.5 rounded-full font-bold text-xs uppercase tracking-widest transition-colors cursor-pointer w-full sm:w-auto">
            {isEn ? 'Delete Profile' : 'Apagar Perfil'}
          </button>
        )}
      </div>

      <div className="mb-10">
        <h1 className="text-3xl md:text-4xl font-black text-slate-900 m-0 tracking-tight">
          {isModoCriacao ? (isEn ? 'New Participant' : 'Novo Participante') : (isEn ? 'Edit Profile' : 'Ficha de Participante')}
        </h1>
        <p className="text-slate-500 mt-2 text-sm md:text-base font-medium">
          {isEn ? 'Keep this data updated to ensure maximum safety during the camps.' : 'Mantenha estes dados atualizados para garantir a máxima segurança dos organizadores.'}
        </p>
      </div>

      <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="flex flex-col gap-10">
        
        {/* BLOCO 1: IDENTIFICAÇÃO */}
        <div className="bg-white p-6 md:p-8 rounded-[2rem] border border-slate-200 shadow-sm">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3 border-b border-slate-100 pb-4 mb-6">
            <span className="text-2xl bg-slate-100 w-10 h-10 flex items-center justify-center rounded-full">👦</span>
            <h2 className="text-base font-black text-slate-900 m-0 uppercase tracking-widest">
              1. {isEn ? 'Basic Identification' : 'Identificação Básica'}
            </h2>
          </div>
          
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-6">
            <div className="md:col-span-2">
              <label className={labelClass}>{isEn ? 'Full Name' : 'Nome Completo'} <span className="text-red-500">*</span></label>
              <input type="text" required value={formData.nome} onChange={e => setFormData({...formData, nome: e.target.value})} className={inputClass} placeholder="Nome do participante" />
            </div>
            <div>
              <label className={labelClass}>{isEn ? 'Date of Birth' : 'Data de Nascimento'} <span className="text-red-500">*</span></label>
              <input type="date" required value={formData.data_nascimento} onChange={e => setFormData({...formData, data_nascimento: e.target.value})} className={inputClass} />
            </div>
            <div>
              <label className={labelClass}>{isEn ? 'Gender' : 'Sexo'} <span className="text-red-500">*</span></label>
              <select required value={formData.sexo} onChange={e => setFormData({...formData, sexo: e.target.value})} className={selectClass}>
                <option value="">Selecione...</option>
                <option value="Masculino">{isEn ? 'Male' : 'Masculino'}</option>
                <option value="Feminino">{isEn ? 'Female' : 'Feminino'}</option>
                <option value="Prefiro não dizer">{isEn ? 'Prefer not to say' : 'Prefiro não dizer'}</option>
              </select>
            </div>
            <div className="md:col-span-2">
              <label className={labelClass}>{isEn ? 'Tax ID (NIF)' : 'NIF da Criança'}</label>
              <input type="text" value={formData.nif} onChange={e => setFormData({...formData, nif: e.target.value})} className={inputClass} placeholder={isEn ? "Optional (For invoices)" : "Opcional (Para emissão de faturas e deduções no IRS)"} />
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* BLOCO 2: CLÍNICO */}
        <div className="bg-red-50 p-6 md:p-8 rounded-[2rem] border border-red-100 shadow-sm">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3 border-b border-red-200/50 pb-4 mb-6">
            <span className="text-2xl bg-white text-red-500 w-10 h-10 flex items-center justify-center rounded-full shadow-sm">🏥</span>
            <h2 className="text-base font-black text-red-900 m-0 uppercase tracking-widest">
              2. {isEn ? 'Medical & Safety Profile' : 'Perfil Clínico e Segurança'}
            </h2>
          </div>
          
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-6">
            <div className="md:w-1/2">
              <label className={`${labelClass} !text-red-800`}>{isEn ? 'Blood Type' : 'Tipo Sanguíneo'}</label>
              <select value={formData.tipo_sanguineo} onChange={e => setFormData({...formData, tipo_sanguineo: e.target.value})} className={`${selectClass} !border-red-200 !bg-white focus:!border-red-500`}>
                <option value="">{isEn ? 'Unknown / Not declared' : 'Desconhecido / Não declarado'}</option>
                <option value="A+">A+</option><option value="A-">A-</option>
                <option value="B+">B+</option><option value="B-">B-</option>
                <option value="AB+">AB+</option><option value="AB-">AB-</option>
                <option value="O+">O+</option><option value="O-">O-</option>
              </select>
            </div>
            <div>
              <label className={`${labelClass} !text-red-800`}>{isEn ? 'Food Allergies or Dietary Restrictions' : 'Alergias ou Restrições Alimentares'}</label>
              <input type="text" value={formData.restricoes_alimentares} onChange={e => setFormData({...formData, restricoes_alimentares: e.target.value})} placeholder={isEn ? 'E.g.: Peanuts, Lactose intolerant, Vegan...' : 'Ex: Amendoins, Intolerante à lactose, Celíaco, Nenhuma...'} className={`${inputClass} !border-red-200 !bg-white focus:!border-red-500`} />
            </div>
            <div>
              <label className={`${labelClass} !text-red-800`}>{isEn ? 'Chronic Diseases or Conditions' : 'Doenças Crónicas ou Condições Médicas'}</label>
              <input type="text" value={formData.doencas_cronicas} onChange={e => setFormData({...formData, doencas_cronicas: e.target.value})} placeholder={isEn ? 'E.g.: Asthma, Diabetes, Heart condition...' : 'Ex: Asma, Diabetes, Nenhuma...'} className={`${inputClass} !border-red-200 !bg-white focus:!border-red-500`} />
            </div>
            <div>
              <label className={`${labelClass} !text-red-800`}>{isEn ? 'Regular Medication' : 'Medicação Regular'}</label>
              <input type="text" value={formData.medicacao_regular} onChange={e => setFormData({...formData, medicacao_regular: e.target.value})} placeholder={isEn ? 'E.g.: Needs inhaler during sports...' : 'Ex: Toma anti-histamínico de manhã...'} className={`${inputClass} !border-red-200 !bg-white focus:!border-red-500`} />
            </div>
            <div>
              <label className={`${labelClass} !text-red-800`}>{isEn ? 'Physical Limitations' : 'Limitações Físicas'}</label>
              <input type="text" value={formData.limitacoes_fisicas} onChange={e => setFormData({...formData, limitacoes_fisicas: e.target.value})} placeholder={isEn ? 'E.g.: Cannot do high-impact sports...' : 'Ex: Não pode fazer desportos de alto impacto...'} className={`${inputClass} !border-red-200 !bg-white focus:!border-red-500`} />
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* BLOCO 3: LOGÍSTICA */}
        <div className="bg-blue-50 p-6 md:p-8 rounded-[2rem] border border-blue-100 shadow-sm">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3 border-b border-blue-200/50 pb-4 mb-6">
            <span className="text-2xl bg-white text-blue-600 w-10 h-10 flex items-center justify-center rounded-full shadow-sm">🎒</span>
            <h2 className="text-base font-black text-blue-900 m-0 uppercase tracking-widest">
              3. {isEn ? 'Skills & Logistics' : 'Competências e Logística'}
            </h2>
          </div>
          
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-6">
            <div>
              <label className={`${labelClass} !text-blue-800`}>{isEn ? 'Knows how to swim?' : 'Sabe nadar?'}</label>
              <select value={formData.sabe_nadar} onChange={e => setFormData({...formData, sabe_nadar: e.target.value})} className={`${selectClass} !border-blue-200 !bg-white focus:!border-blue-500`}>
                <option value="">{isEn ? 'Not answered' : 'Não respondido'}</option>
                <option value="Sim, perfeitamente">{isEn ? 'Yes, perfectly' : 'Sim, perfeitamente'}</option>
                <option value="O básico (com pé)">{isEn ? 'Basics only' : 'O básico (precisa de pé)'}</option>
                <option value="Não">{isEn ? 'No' : 'Não'}</option>
              </select>
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className={`${labelClass} !text-blue-800`}>{isEn ? 'Rides a bicycle?' : 'Sabe andar de bicicleta?'}</label>
              <select value={formData.sabe_andar_bicicleta} onChange={e => setFormData({...formData, sabe_andar_bicicleta: e.target.value})} className={`${selectClass} !border-blue-200 !bg-white focus:!border-blue-500`}>
                <option value="">{isEn ? 'Not answered' : 'Não respondido'}</option>
                <option value="Sim">{isEn ? 'Yes' : 'Sim'}</option>
                <option value="Sim, mas com rodinhas">{isEn ? 'Yes, with training wheels' : 'Sim, mas com rodinhas'}</option>
                <option value="Não">{isEn ? 'No' : 'Não'}</option>
              </select>
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className={`${labelClass} !text-blue-800`}>{isEn ? 'T-Shirt Size (For gifts)' : 'Tamanho de T-Shirt (Brindes)'}</label>
              <select value={formData.tamanho_tshirt} onChange={e => setFormData({...formData, tamanho_tshirt: e.target.value})} className={`${selectClass} !border-blue-200 !bg-white focus:!border-blue-500`}>
                <option value="">{isEn ? 'Not answered' : 'Não respondido'}</option>
                <option value="5-6 Anos">5-6 Anos</option>
                <option value="7-8 Anos">7-8 Anos</option>
                <option value="9-11 Anos">9-11 Anos</option>
                <option value="12-14 Anos">12-14 Anos</option>
                <option value="S Adulto">S (Adulto)</option>
                <option value="M Adulto">M (Adulto)</option>
                <option value="L Adulto">L (Adulto)</option>
              </select>
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className={`${labelClass} !text-blue-800`}>{isEn ? 'English Level' : 'Nível de Inglês'}</label>
              <select value={formData.nivel_ingles} onChange={e => setFormData({...formData, nivel_ingles: e.target.value})} className={`${selectClass} !border-blue-200 !bg-white focus:!border-blue-500`}>
                <option value="">{isEn ? 'Not answered' : 'Não respondido'}</option>
                <option value="Nenhum">Nenhum</option>
                <option value="Básico">Básico</option>
                <option value="Intermédio">Intermédio</option>
                <option value="Avançado / Fluente">Avançado / Fluente</option>
              </select>
            </div>

            <div className="md:col-span-2">
              <label className={`${labelClass} !text-blue-800`}>{isEn ? 'Fears or Phobias' : 'Fobias ou Medos Conhecidos'}</label>
              <input type="text" value={formData.fobias_medos} onChange={e => setFormData({...formData, fobias_medos: e.target.value})} placeholder={isEn ? 'E.g.: Fear of dogs, heights, darkness...' : 'Ex: Medo de alturas, água funda, escuro...'} className={`${inputClass} !border-blue-200 !bg-white focus:!border-blue-500`} />
            </div>

            <div className="md:col-span-2">
              <label className={`${labelClass} !text-blue-800`}>{isEn ? 'Personality Traits' : 'Perfil Comportamental / Personalidade'}</label>
              <input type="text" value={formData.perfil_comportamental} onChange={e => setFormData({...formData, perfil_comportamental: e.target.value})} placeholder={isEn ? 'E.g.: Very shy, energetic, natural leader...' : 'Ex: Criança tímida, muito ativa, necessita de rotinas...'} className={`${inputClass} !border-blue-200 !bg-white focus:!border-blue-500`} />
            </div>

            <div className="md:col-span-2 p-5 bg-white rounded-xl border border-blue-200 mt-2 shadow-sm">
              <label className="flex items-center gap-4 cursor-pointer group">
                <input type="checkbox" checked={formData.autorizacao_fotografia === 'Sim'} onChange={e => setFormData({...formData, autorizacao_fotografia: e.target.checked ? 'Sim' : 'Não'})} className="w-6 h-6 accent-[#0E3A68] flex-shrink-0 cursor-pointer" />
                <span className="text-sm text-blue-900 font-bold group-hover:text-[#0E3A68] transition-colors leading-relaxed">
                  {isEn ? 'I authorize the capture of images/video for the camp’s internal updates and marketing.' : 'Autorizo a captação de imagem/vídeo exclusiva para atualizações de segurança aos pais e marketing do campo.'}
                </span>
              </label>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        <button 
          type="submit" 
          disabled={saving} 
          className="w-full mt-4 p-5 bg-[#0E3A68] text-white font-black uppercase tracking-widest rounded-[1.5rem] border-none cursor-pointer text-base hover:bg-[#0b2b4d] transition-all shadow-xl shadow-[#0E3A68]/30 disabled:opacity-50 hover:-translate-y-1"
        >
          {saving ? (isEn ? 'Saving profile...' : 'A guardar perfil...') : (isEn ? 'Save Security Profile' : 'Gravar Ficha de Participante')}
        </button>
      </form>
    </div>
  );
}
</file>

<file path="app/[lang]/(public)/cliente/criancas/page.tsx">
"use client";

import { useEffect, useState, use } from "react";
import { supabase } from "@/lib/supabase";
import Link from "next/link";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import React from "react";

export default function ListaCriancas({ params }: { params: Promise<{ lang: string }> }) {
  const { lang } = use(params);
  const isEn = lang === 'en';
  const router = useRouter();

  const [criancas, setCriancas] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);

  useEffect(() => {
    const fetchCriancas = async () => {
      const { data: { session } } = await supabase.auth.getSession();
      if (!session) return;
      const { data } = await supabase
        .from('criancas')
        .select('*')
        .eq('cliente_id', session.user.id)
        .order('created_at', { ascending: false });
      
      setCriancas(data || []);
      setLoading(false);
    };
    fetchCriancas();
  }, []);

  const handleCriarNovaCrianca = () => {
    router.push(`/${lang}/cliente/criancas/novo`);
  };

  const calcularIdade = (dataNasc: string) => {
    if (!dataNasc) return null;
    const diff = Date.now() - new Date(dataNasc).getTime();
    return Math.abs(new Date(diff).getUTCFullYear() - 1970);
  };

  return (
    <div className="max-w-[1000px] mx-auto pb-20 font-sans">
      
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row justify-between items-start sm:items-center gap-5 mb-10">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-3xl md:text-4xl font-black text-slate-900 m-0 tracking-tight">
            {isEn ? 'My Children' : 'Os Meus Filhos'}
          </h1>
          <p className="text-slate-500 mt-2 text-sm md:text-base font-medium">
            {isEn ? 'Manage participant profiles for faster bookings.' : 'Gira os perfis dos participantes para reservas mais rápidas e seguras.'}
          </p>
        </div>
        
        <button onClick={handleCriarNovaCrianca} className="w-full sm:w-auto bg-[#0E3A68] hover:bg-[#0b2b4d] text-white px-6 py-3.5 rounded-xl font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-xs transition-all shadow-md hover:-translate-y-0.5">
          + {isEn ? 'Add Child' : 'Adicionar Participante'}
        </button>
      </div>

      {loading ? (
        <div className="text-center py-20 text-slate-400 font-bold text-sm uppercase tracking-widest animate-pulse">
          {isEn ? 'Loading profiles...' : 'A carregar perfis familiares...'}
        </div>
      ) : criancas.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="text-center py-16 md:py-24 px-6 bg-white border-2 border-dashed border-slate-300 rounded-[2rem] shadow-sm">
          <span className="text-5xl block mb-6 opacity-50">👦</span>
          <p className="text-slate-500 mb-6 text-base md:text-lg font-medium">{isEn ? 'No children profiles found yet.' : 'Ainda não adicionou o perfil do seu filho(a).'}</p>
          <button onClick={handleCriarNovaCrianca} className="bg-[#e2a41d] text-white px-8 py-4 rounded-xl font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-xs shadow-md hover:bg-[#d4991b] transition-all cursor-pointer">
            {isEn ? 'Create profile now' : 'Criar Primeiro Perfil'}
          </button>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-6">
          {criancas.map(c => {
            const idade = calcularIdade(c.data_nascimento);
            
            return (
              <Link key={c.id} href={`/${lang}/cliente/criancas/${c.id}`} className="bg-white p-6 md:p-8 rounded-[1.5rem] border border-slate-200 no-underline text-inherit flex flex-col shadow-sm hover:shadow-lg hover:border-[#0E3A68] transition-all duration-300 group">
                <div className="flex justify-between items-start mb-6">
                  <h3 className="m-0 text-2xl font-black text-slate-900 leading-tight truncate pr-2 group-hover:text-[#0E3A68] transition-colors">{c.nome}</h3>
                  <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-full bg-slate-50 border border-slate-100 flex items-center justify-center text-slate-400 group-hover:bg-[#0E3A68] group-hover:text-white group-hover:border-[#0E3A68] transition-all shadow-sm">
                    ✎
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div className="flex gap-2 flex-wrap mb-8">
                  {idade !== null ? (
                    <span className="bg-slate-100 text-slate-700 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold border border-slate-200 shadow-sm">🎂 {idade} {isEn ? 'years' : 'anos'}</span>
                  ) : (
                    <span className="bg-amber-50 text-amber-700 border border-amber-200 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-[10px] uppercase tracking-widest font-black shadow-sm">⚠️ Idade em falta</span>
                  )}
                  {c.sexo && c.sexo !== 'Prefiro não dizer' && (
                    <span className="bg-slate-100 text-slate-700 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold border border-slate-200 shadow-sm">👤 {c.sexo}</span>
                  )}
                </div>

                <div className="mt-auto pt-5 border-t border-slate-100">
                  {c.restricoes_alimentares || c.doencas_cronicas ? (
                    <div className="p-3.5 bg-red-50 border border-red-200 text-red-800 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold flex items-center gap-3 shadow-inner">
                      <span className="text-lg">⚠️</span> 
                      <span className="font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-[10px]">{isEn ? 'Active Medical Alerts' : 'Alertas Clínicos Ativos'}</span>
                    </div>
                  ) : (
                    <div className="p-3.5 bg-emerald-50 border border-emerald-100 text-emerald-700 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold flex items-center gap-3 shadow-inner">
                      <span className="text-lg text-emerald-500">✅</span> 
                      <span className="font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-[10px]">{isEn ? 'No health restrictions' : 'Sem restrições de saúde'}</span>
                    </div>
                  )}
                </div>
              </Link>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
</file>

<file path="app/[lang]/(public)/cliente/dashboard/page.tsx">
"use client";

import { useEffect, useState, use } from "react";
import { supabase } from "@/lib/supabase";
import Link from "next/link";
import React from "react";

export default function DashboardCliente({ params }: { params: Promise<{ lang: string }> }) {
  const { lang } = use(params);
  const isEn = lang === 'en';

  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [loadingStripe, setLoadingStripe] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const [perfilPai, setPerfilPai] = useState<any>(null);
  const [dadosEmFalta, setDadosEmFalta] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [isPerfilModalOpen, setIsPerfilModalOpen] = useState(false);
  const [savingPerfil, setSavingPerfil] = useState(false);
  const [perfilForm, setPerfilForm] = useState({
    nome_completo: "", nif: "", telefone: "", contacto_emergencia: ""
  });

  const [reservas, setReservas] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [wishlists, setWishlists] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [sugestoesAzuis, setSugestoesAzuis] = useState<any[]>([]);

  const [reservaModal, setReservaModal] = useState<any>(null);
  const [cancelState, setCancelState] = useState({ loading: false, confirm: false });
  const [changeRequest, setChangeRequest] = useState({ active: false, text: "", loading: false });

  const [isShareModalOpen, setIsShareModalOpen] = useState(false);
  const [shareUrl, setShareUrl] = useState("");
  const [shareTitle, setShareTitle] = useState("");
  const [copied, setCopied] = useState(false);

  const verificarFaltas = (dados: any) => {
    const faltas = [];
    if (!dados.nome_completo || dados.nome_completo.trim() === "") faltas.push(isEn ? 'Full Name' : 'Nome do Encarregado de Educação');
    if (!dados.nif || dados.nif.trim() === "") faltas.push('NIF (Para Faturação)');
    if (!dados.telefone || dados.telefone.trim() === "") faltas.push(isEn ? 'Phone Number' : 'Número de Telemóvel');
    if (!dados.contacto_emergencia || dados.contacto_emergencia.trim() === "") faltas.push(isEn ? 'Emergency Contact' : 'Contacto de Emergência Alternativo');
    return faltas;
  };

  const fetchData = async () => {
    const { data: { session } } = await supabase.auth.getSession();
    if (!session) return;

    const userId = session.user.id;

    const { data: perfilData } = await supabase.from('perfis').select('*').eq('id', userId).single();
    const { data: reservasData } = await supabase.from('reservas').select('*').eq('cliente_id', userId).order('created_at', { ascending: false });
    
    const { data: camposData } = await supabase
      .from('campos')
      .select('id, nome, nome_en, imagem, local, local_en, organizador_id, preco, politica_cancelamento')
      .eq('status_aprovacao', 'Aprovado')
      .eq('ativo', true);

    const { data: criancasData } = await supabase.from('criancas').select('*').eq('cliente_id', userId);
    const { data: wishData } = await supabase.from('wishlists').select('id, nome, token_partilha').eq('user_id', userId).order('created_at', { ascending: false });

    if (perfilData) {
      setPerfilPai(perfilData);
      setPerfilForm({
        nome_completo: perfilData.nome_completo || "",
        nif: perfilData.nif || "",
        telefone: perfilData.telefone || "",
        contacto_emergencia: perfilData.contacto_emergencia || ""
      });
      setDadosEmFalta(verificarFaltas(perfilData));
    }

    const camposCompradosIds: string[] = [];
    if (reservasData) {
      const idsDeCamposReservados = Array.from(new Set(reservasData.map(r => r.campo_id)));
      
      const { data: todosCamposHistorico } = await supabase
        .from('campos')
        .select('id, nome, nome_en, imagem, local, local_en, organizador_id, preco, politica_cancelamento')
        .in('id', idsDeCamposReservados);

      const reservasCruzadas = reservasData.map(res => {
        const campo = todosCamposHistorico?.find(c => c.id === res.campo_id) || {};
        const crianca = criancasData?.find(cr => cr.id === res.crianca_id) || {};
        camposCompradosIds.push(res.campo_id);
        return { ...res, campos: campo, criancas: crianca };
      });
      setReservas(reservasCruzadas);
    }

    if (camposData) {
      const recomendacoes = camposData.filter(c => !camposCompradosIds.includes(c.id)).slice(0, 3);
      setSugestoesAzuis(recomendacoes);
    }

    setWishlists(wishData || []);
    setLoading(false);
  };

  useEffect(() => { fetchData(); }, [lang, isEn]);

  const handleSavePerfil = async () => {
    if (!perfilForm.nome_completo || !perfilForm.nif || !perfilForm.telefone || !perfilForm.contacto_emergencia) {
      alert(isEn ? "Please fill in all required fields." : "Por favor preencha todos os campos obrigatórios.");
      return;
    }
    setSavingPerfil(true);

    const { error } = await supabase.from('perfis').update({
        nome_completo: perfilForm.nome_completo, nif: perfilForm.nif,
        telefone: perfilForm.telefone, contacto_emergencia: perfilForm.contacto_emergencia
    }).eq('id', perfilPai.id);

    if (error) alert((isEn ? "Error updating profile: " : "Erro ao atualizar perfil: ") + error.message);
    else {
      setPerfilPai((prev: any) => ({ ...prev, ...perfilForm }));
      setDadosEmFalta(verificarFaltas(perfilForm));
      setIsPerfilModalOpen(false);
      alert(isEn ? "Profile details saved successfully." : "Dados guardados com sucesso.");
    }
    setSavingPerfil(false);
  };

  const handlePagarRestante = async (reserva: any, valorFaltaCobrar: number) => {
    setLoadingStripe(reserva.id);
    try {
      const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser();
      const { data: orgData } = await supabase.from('perfis').select('stripe_account_id').eq('id', reserva.campos.organizador_id).single();

      const res = await fetch('/api/stripe-checkout', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          reservasIds: [reserva.id],
          totalAmount: valorFaltaCobrar, 
          userEmail: user?.email,
          lang: lang,
          campoNome: isEn && reserva.campos.nome_en ? reserva.campos.nome_en : reserva.campos.nome,
          stripeAccountId: orgData?.stripe_account_id,
          tipoPagamento: 'pagamento_final'
        })
      });

      if (!res.ok) throw new Error("Erro no processamento.");
      const data = await res.json();
      if (data.url) window.location.href = data.url;
    } catch (err: any) { alert("Erro ao iniciar pagamento: " + err.message); setLoadingStripe(null); }
  };

  const handleCancelarReserva = async (reserva: any) => {
    setCancelState({ ...cancelState, loading: true });
    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/cancelar-reserva', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ reservaId: reserva.id })
      });

      const data = await res.json();
      if (!res.ok) throw new Error(data.error || "Erro ao cancelar a inscrição.");

      alert(isEn ? "Booking successfully cancelled." : "Inscrição cancelada com sucesso.");
      setReservaModal(null);
      setCancelState({ loading: false, confirm: false });
      fetchData(); 
    } catch (err: any) { alert(err.message); setCancelState({ loading: false, confirm: false }); }
  };

  const handleEnviarPedidoAlteracao = async (reserva: any) => {
    if (!changeRequest.text.trim()) return alert(isEn ? "Please describe your request." : "Por favor, descreva a alteração que pretende.");
    
    setChangeRequest(prev => ({ ...prev, loading: true }));
    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/inbox/send-change-request', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          reservaId: reserva.id, organizadorId: reserva.campos.organizador_id,
          campoNome: isEn && reserva.campos.nome_en ? reserva.campos.nome_en : reserva.campos.nome,
          mensagem: changeRequest.text, lang: lang
        })
      });

      if (!res.ok) throw new Error("Erro no envio do pedido.");

      alert(isEn ? "Change request sent! The organizer will review it." : "Pedido enviado com sucesso! O organizador irá rever a sua solicitação.");
      setChangeRequest({ active: false, text: "", loading: false });
    } catch (err: any) { alert((isEn ? "Error sending request: " : "Erro ao enviar pedido: ") + err.message); setChangeRequest(prev => ({ ...prev, loading: false })); }
  };

  const abrirModalPartilha = (token: string, nomeLista: string) => {
    const url = `${window.location.origin}/${lang}/lista/${token}`;
    setShareUrl(url); setShareTitle(nomeLista); setCopied(false); setIsShareModalOpen(true);
  };

  const handleCopyLink = async () => {
    try { await navigator.clipboard.writeText(shareUrl); setCopied(true); setTimeout(() => setCopied(false), 2000); } catch (err) {}
  };

  const fecharReservaModal = () => {
    setReservaModal(null);
    setCancelState({ loading: false, confirm: false });
    setChangeRequest({ active: false, text: "", loading: false });
  };

  if (loading) return <div className="p-12 text-center text-slate-500 font-bold">{isEn ? 'Loading your dashboard...' : 'A organizar a sua área pessoal...'}</div>;

  return (
    <div className="max-w-[1000px] mx-auto font-sans relative pb-20">
      
      {/* CABEÇALHO */}
      <div className="mb-8">
        <h1 className="text-3xl md:text-4xl font-black text-slate-900 m-0 tracking-tight">
          {isEn ? 'Upcoming Camps' : 'Próximos Campos'}
        </h1>
        <p className="text-slate-500 mt-2 text-sm md:text-base font-medium">
          {isEn ? 'View your active programs and child schedules.' : 'Acompanhe as inscrições e a logística das próximas férias.'}
        </p>
      </div>

      {/* ALERTA DE DADOS EM FALTA NO PERFIL */}
      {dadosEmFalta.length > 0 && (
        <div className="bg-amber-50 border border-amber-200 rounded-[1.5rem] p-6 mb-10 shadow-sm">
          <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-start gap-4">
            <div className="text-amber-500 text-3xl mt-1">⚠️</div>
            <div>
              <h3 className="text-amber-800 font-bold text-lg mb-1">{isEn ? 'Missing Important Information' : 'Dados do Encarregado Incompletos'}</h3>
              <p className="text-amber-700 text-sm mb-3">
                {isEn ? 'To ensure safety and proper invoicing, please add the following data to your profile:' : 'Para garantir o contacto do organizador em caso de emergência e a emissão correta da sua fatura, preencha os seguintes dados:'}
              </p>
              <ul className="list-disc pl-5 text-amber-700 text-sm font-bold mb-4">
                {dadosEmFalta.map((falta, idx) => <li key={idx}>{falta}</li>)}
              </ul>
              <button 
                onClick={() => setIsPerfilModalOpen(true)}
                className="bg-[#0E3A68] hover:bg-[#0b2b4d] text-white px-6 py-3 rounded-xl font-bold text-sm transition-colors shadow-sm cursor-pointer w-full sm:w-auto uppercase tracking-widest"
              >
                {isEn ? 'Complete Profile Now' : 'Completar Perfil Agora'}
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* SECÇÃO 1: RESERVAS */}
      {reservas.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="text-center p-10 md:p-20 bg-white border-2 border-dashed border-slate-300 rounded-[2rem] mb-12 shadow-sm">
          <span className="text-5xl block mb-4 opacity-50">🏕️</span>
          <p className="text-slate-500 text-base md:text-lg mb-6 font-bold">{isEn ? 'No camp enrollments found yet.' : 'Ainda não inscreveu nenhum participante em programas de férias.'}</p>
          <Link href={`/${lang}/pesquisa`} className="inline-block bg-[#0E3A68] text-white px-8 py-4 rounded-xl font-black uppercase tracking-widest shadow-md hover:bg-[#0b2b4d] hover:-translate-y-0.5 transition-all text-sm">
            {isEn ? 'Browse Camps' : 'Encontrar Programas'}
          </Link>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-6 mb-16">
          {reservas.map((reserva) => {
            const campo = reserva.campos;
            const nomeCampo = isEn && campo?.nome_en ? campo.nome_en : campo?.nome;
            const localCampo = isEn && campo?.local_en ? campo.local_en : campo?.local;
            
            const isReembolsada = reserva.status_pagamento === 'Reembolsado' || reserva.status_reembolso === 'Reembolsado';
            const isAguardandoExtra = reserva.status_pagamento === 'Aguardando Pagamento Extra';
            const isSinalPago = !isReembolsada && reserva.status_pagamento === 'Sinal Pago';
            const isPago = !isReembolsada && reserva.status_pagamento === 'Pago';
            const isPendente = !isReembolsada && !isSinalPago && !isPago && !isAguardandoExtra;
            
            const valorTotal = Number(reserva.valor_total) || 0;
            const valorJaPago = Number(reserva.valor_pago) || 0;
            const valorFalta = valorTotal - valorJaPago;

            return (
              <div key={reserva.id} className="group flex flex-col bg-white rounded-[1.5rem] border border-slate-200 overflow-hidden shadow-sm relative transition-all duration-300 hover:shadow-xl">
                
                <button 
                  onClick={() => setReservaModal(reserva)} 
                  className="absolute inset-0 z-10 w-full h-full cursor-pointer focus:outline-none"
                  aria-label="Ver Detalhes"
                />
                
                <div className={`h-40 md:h-48 w-full relative overflow-hidden bg-slate-100 ${isReembolsada ? 'grayscale' : ''}`}>
                  <img src={campo?.imagem || 'https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1502680390469-be75c86b636f?q=80&w=600'} alt={nomeCampo} className="w-full h-full object-cover transition-transform duration-700 group-hover:scale-105" />
                  <div className="absolute top-4 right-4 bg-slate-900/80 backdrop-blur-sm px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-white text-[10px] font-black tracking-widest uppercase shadow-sm">
                    {reserva.turno_nome || 'Turno'}
                  </div>
                  {isReembolsada && (
                    <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-red-900/40 flex items-center justify-center">
                      <span className="bg-red-600 text-white font-black uppercase tracking-widest px-4 py-2 rounded-xl text-sm transform -rotate-12 shadow-lg border-2 border-red-400">
                        {isEn ? 'Cancelled' : 'Cancelada'}
                      </span>
                    </div>
                  )}
                </div>

                <div className={`p-5 md:p-6 flex flex-col flex-1 relative z-20 ${isReembolsada ? 'opacity-60' : ''}`}>
                  <h3 className="text-lg md:text-xl font-black text-slate-900 m-0 leading-tight group-hover:text-[#0E3A68] transition-colors pointer-events-none">{nomeCampo}</h3>
                  <p className="text-[11px] md:text-xs font-bold text-slate-500 mt-2 mb-6 pointer-events-none">📍 {localCampo}</p>
                  
                  <div className="border-t border-slate-100 pt-4 mt-auto flex justify-between items-end mb-4 pointer-events-none">
                    <div>
                      <span className="block text-[9px] text-slate-400 font-black uppercase tracking-widest mb-1">{isEn ? 'PARTICIPANT' : 'PARTICIPANTE'}</span>
                      <span className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-700">👦 {reserva.criancas?.nome || 'N/D'}</span>
                    </div>
                    <div className="text-right">
                      <span className="block text-[9px] text-slate-400 font-black uppercase tracking-widest mb-1">TOTAL</span>
                      <span className={`text-lg font-black leading-none ${isReembolsada ? 'text-slate-400 line-through' : 'text-[#0E3A68]'}`}>
                        {valorTotal}€
                      </span>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div className="px-5 md:px-6 pb-5 md:pb-6 relative z-30">
                  {isReembolsada ? (
                    <div className="bg-red-50 border border-red-200 rounded-xl p-3 text-center">
                      <span className="text-[10px] md:text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-red-600">❌ Inscrição Anulada</span>
                    </div>
                  ) : isAguardandoExtra && valorFalta > 0 ? (
                    <div className="bg-blue-50 border border-blue-200 rounded-xl p-4 flex items-center justify-between shadow-sm">
                      <div>
                        <p className="text-[9px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-blue-700 m-0">Ajuste Pendente</p>
                        <p className="text-base font-black text-blue-800 m-0">{valorFalta.toFixed(2)}€</p>
                      </div>
                      <button 
                        onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); handlePagarRestante(reserva, valorFalta); }}
                        disabled={loadingStripe === reserva.id}
                        className="bg-[#0E3A68] text-white font-bold text-xs px-4 py-2.5 rounded-lg hover:bg-[#0b2b4d] transition-colors disabled:opacity-50 shadow-sm uppercase tracking-widest"
                      >
                        {loadingStripe === reserva.id ? 'A processar...' : 'Pagar Alteração'}
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  ) : isSinalPago && valorFalta > 0 ? (
                    <div className="bg-amber-50 border border-amber-200 rounded-xl p-4 flex items-center justify-between shadow-sm">
                      <div>
                        <p className="text-[9px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-amber-700 m-0">Falta Pagar</p>
                        <p className="text-base font-black text-amber-800 m-0">{valorFalta.toFixed(2)}€</p>
                      </div>
                      <button 
                        onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); handlePagarRestante(reserva, valorFalta); }}
                        disabled={loadingStripe === reserva.id}
                        className="bg-[#e2a41d] text-white font-bold text-xs px-4 py-2.5 rounded-lg hover:bg-[#d4991b] transition-colors disabled:opacity-50 shadow-sm uppercase tracking-widest"
                      >
                        {loadingStripe === reserva.id ? 'A processar...' : 'Pagar Restante'}
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  ) : isPago ? (
                    <div className="bg-[#0E3A68]/5 border border-[#0E3A68]/10 rounded-xl p-3 text-center">
                      <span className="text-[10px] md:text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-[#0E3A68]">✓ Vaga Confirmada (100% Pago)</span>
                    </div>
                  ) : isPendente ? (
                    <div className="bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl p-3 text-center">
                      <span className="text-[10px] md:text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-slate-500">⏳ Pagamento Pendente</span>
                    </div>
                  ) : null}
                </div>
              </div>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      )}

      {/* MÓDULO DE SUGESTÕES CROSS-SELLING */}
      {sugestoesAzuis.length > 0 && (
        <div className="bg-[#0E3A68] border border-[#0E3A68] rounded-[2rem] p-6 md:p-10 mb-16 relative overflow-hidden shadow-xl">
          <div className="absolute top-0 right-0 w-64 h-64 bg-[#e2a41d] rounded-full blur-3xl opacity-20 -mr-20 -mt-20 pointer-events-none"></div>
          <h2 className="text-2xl font-black text-white mb-2 relative z-10">{isEn ? 'Suggested for You' : 'Descubra Mais Aventuras'}</h2>
          <p className="text-blue-200 font-medium text-sm mb-6 relative z-10 max-w-xl">
            {isEn ? 'Explore these highly-rated programs that you haven\'t booked yet.' : 'Explore estes programas altamente recomendados para o próximo ano. Excluímos automaticamente as suas reservas atuais.'}
          </p>
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-4 relative z-10">
            {sugestoesAzuis.map(sug => (
              <Link key={sug.id} href={`/${lang}/campo/${sug.id}`} className="bg-white/10 rounded-2xl p-3 border border-white/20 hover:border-[#e2a41d] hover:bg-white/20 transition-all group flex items-center gap-3 no-underline">
                <img src={sug.imagem || 'https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1502680390469-be75c86b636f?q=80&w=200'} alt={sug.nome} className="w-12 h-12 rounded-xl object-cover shadow-sm" />
                <div className="min-w-0">
                  <h4 className="font-bold text-white text-sm leading-tight truncate group-hover:text-[#e2a41d] transition-colors">{isEn && sug.nome_en ? sug.nome_en : sug.nome}</h4>
                  <p className="text-[10px] text-blue-200 mt-1 font-bold uppercase tracking-widest truncate">{isEn && sug.local_en ? sug.local_en : sug.local}</p>
                </div>
              </Link>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* SECÇÃO 2: WISHLISTS */}
      <div>
        <h2 className="text-2xl font-black text-slate-900 mb-6 tracking-tight">{isEn ? 'Your Wishlists' : 'As Suas Listas de Férias'}</h2>
        {wishlists.length === 0 ? (
          <p className="text-slate-500 font-medium text-sm bg-white p-6 rounded-2xl border border-dashed border-slate-300 text-center">{isEn ? 'You have no saved lists yet. Tap the heart on any camp to create one!' : 'Ainda não tem listas. Clique no coração em qualquer campo nas pesquisas para começar a organizar as férias!'}</p>
        ) : (
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
            {wishlists.map((lista) => (
              <div key={lista.id} className="bg-white p-6 rounded-[1.5rem] border border-slate-200 shadow-sm flex flex-col justify-between h-40 relative group hover:border-[#0E3A68]/30 transition-colors">
                <div>
                  <h3 className="font-black text-slate-900 text-lg mb-1 line-clamp-2">{lista.nome}</h3>
                </div>
                <div className="flex justify-between items-center mt-4">
                  <Link href={`/${lang}/lista/${lista.token_partilha}`} className="text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-[#0E3A68] hover:text-[#e2a41d] transition-colors no-underline">
                    {isEn ? 'View list' : 'Ver lista'} &rarr;
                  </Link>
                  <button onClick={() => abrirModalPartilha(lista.token_partilha, lista.nome)} className="w-10 h-10 rounded-full bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 flex items-center justify-center text-slate-500 hover:bg-[#0E3A68] hover:text-white transition-colors shadow-sm">
                    <svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="16" height="16" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="2.5" strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round"><circle cx="18" cy="5" r="3"></circle><circle cx="6" cy="12" r="3"></circle><circle cx="18" cy="19" r="3"></circle><line x1="8.59" y1="13.51" x2="15.42" y2="17.49"></line><line x1="15.41" y1="6.51" x2="8.59" y2="10.49"></line></svg>
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* LIGHTBOX: COMPLETAR PERFIL */}
      {isPerfilModalOpen && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 bg-slate-900/80 backdrop-blur-sm transition-opacity" onClick={() => setIsPerfilModalOpen(false)}>
          <div className="bg-white rounded-[2rem] w-full max-w-md shadow-2xl overflow-hidden flex flex-col transform transition-transform" onClick={e => e.stopPropagation()}>
            <div className="p-6 border-b border-slate-100 flex items-center justify-between bg-slate-50">
              <div>
                <h3 className="font-black text-slate-900 text-xl m-0 tracking-tight">{isEn ? 'Complete Profile' : 'Dados do Encarregado'}</h3>
                <p className="text-[11px] font-bold uppercase tracking-widest text-slate-500 mt-1 mb-0">{isEn ? 'Fill in missing details.' : 'Informações obrigatórias exigidas.'}</p>
              </div>
              <button onClick={() => setIsPerfilModalOpen(false)} className="w-10 h-10 flex items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-white border border-slate-200 text-slate-500 hover:bg-slate-900 hover:text-white transition-colors font-black">
                &times;
              </button>
            </div>
            <div className="p-6 flex flex-col gap-5">
              <div>
                <label className="block text-[10px] font-black text-slate-500 uppercase tracking-widest mb-2">{isEn ? 'Full Name' : 'Nome Completo Encarregado'}</label>
                <input type="text" required value={perfilForm.nome_completo} onChange={e => setPerfilForm({...perfilForm, nome_completo: e.target.value})} className="w-full p-3.5 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl text-sm font-bold text-slate-900 outline-none focus:border-[#0E3A68] bg-slate-50" />
              </div>
              <div>
                <label className="block text-[10px] font-black text-slate-500 uppercase tracking-widest mb-2">{isEn ? 'Tax ID / NIF' : 'NIF (Para Emissão de Faturas)'}</label>
                <input type="text" required value={perfilForm.nif} onChange={e => setPerfilForm({...perfilForm, nif: e.target.value})} className="w-full p-3.5 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl text-sm font-bold text-slate-900 outline-none focus:border-[#0E3A68] bg-slate-50" />
              </div>
              <div>
                <label className="block text-[10px] font-black text-slate-500 uppercase tracking-widest mb-2">{isEn ? 'Phone Number' : 'Telemóvel Contacto Direto'}</label>
                <input type="tel" required value={perfilForm.telefone} onChange={e => setPerfilForm({...perfilForm, telefone: e.target.value})} className="w-full p-3.5 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl text-sm font-bold text-slate-900 outline-none focus:border-[#0E3A68] bg-slate-50" />
              </div>
              <div>
                <label className="block text-[10px] font-black text-slate-500 uppercase tracking-widest mb-2">{isEn ? 'Emergency Contact' : 'Contacto de Emergência Alternativo'}</label>
                <input type="tel" required value={perfilForm.contacto_emergencia} onChange={e => setPerfilForm({...perfilForm, contacto_emergencia: e.target.value})} className="w-full p-3.5 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl text-sm font-bold text-slate-900 outline-none focus:border-[#0E3A68] bg-slate-50" placeholder="Ex: Contacto dos Avós" />
              </div>
              <button onClick={handleSavePerfil} disabled={savingPerfil} className="w-full p-4 bg-[#0E3A68] hover:bg-[#0b2b4d] text-white font-black uppercase tracking-widest rounded-xl text-xs transition-colors mt-4 disabled:opacity-50 shadow-md shadow-[#0E3A68]/20">
                {savingPerfil ? (isEn ? 'Saving data...' : 'A gravar dados...') : (isEn ? 'Save and Update Profile' : 'Gravar Perfil Agora')}
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* MODAL DETALHADO DA RESERVA E CANCELAMENTO/ALTERAÇÃO */}
      {reservaModal && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 bg-slate-900/80 backdrop-blur-sm transition-opacity" onClick={fecharReservaModal}>
          <div className="bg-white rounded-[2rem] w-full max-w-2xl max-h-[90vh] flex flex-col overflow-hidden shadow-2xl" onClick={e => e.stopPropagation()}>
            <div className="p-6 md:p-8 border-b border-slate-100 flex items-center justify-between bg-slate-50">
              <div>
                <h3 className="font-black text-slate-900 text-xl md:text-2xl m-0 tracking-tight">{isEn ? 'Booking Summary' : 'Resumo da Inscrição'}</h3>
                <p className="text-[10px] text-slate-500 font-bold uppercase tracking-widest mt-1.5 mb-0">Ref: {reservaModal.id}</p>
              </div>
              <button onClick={fecharReservaModal} className="w-10 h-10 flex items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-white border border-slate-200 text-slate-500 hover:bg-slate-900 hover:text-white transition-colors font-black text-xl shadow-sm">
                &times;
              </button>
            </div>
            
            <div className="p-6 md:p-8 overflow-y-auto bg-white flex flex-col gap-8">
              
              {/* STATUS FINANCEIRO TOPO */}
              <div className="bg-[#0E3A68] rounded-[1.5rem] p-6 text-white shadow-lg shadow-[#0E3A68]/20">
                <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-6">
                  <span className="text-[10px] font-black text-blue-200 uppercase tracking-widest">{isEn ? 'Financial Status' : 'Situação Financeira'}</span>
                  <span className={`px-4 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-[9px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest shadow-sm border ${reservaModal.status_pagamento === 'Reembolsado' || reservaModal.status_reembolso === 'Reembolsado' ? 'bg-red-500/20 text-red-200 border-red-500/30' : (reservaModal.status_pagamento === 'Aguardando Pagamento Extra' ? 'bg-blue-500/20 text-blue-200 border-blue-500/30' : 'bg-emerald-500/20 text-emerald-200 border-emerald-500/30')}`}>
                    {reservaModal.status_pagamento === 'Reembolsado' || reservaModal.status_reembolso === 'Reembolsado' ? 'Cancelada' : reservaModal.status_pagamento || 'Pendente'}
                  </span>
                </div>
                <div className="flex justify-between items-end border-t border-blue-800 pt-5">
                  <div>
                    <p className="text-[10px] uppercase tracking-widest text-blue-200 font-black mb-1">{isEn ? 'Amount Paid' : 'Valor já liquidado'}</p>
                    <p className="text-3xl font-black m-0 leading-none">{reservaModal.valor_pago || 0}€</p>
                  </div>
                  <div className="text-right">
                    <p className="text-[10px] uppercase tracking-widest text-blue-200 font-black mb-1">{isEn ? 'Total Cost' : 'Custo Total'}</p>
                    <p className={`text-xl font-bold m-0 leading-none ${reservaModal.status_pagamento === 'Reembolsado' || reservaModal.status_reembolso === 'Reembolsado' ? 'line-through text-blue-400' : 'text-blue-100'}`}>
                      {reservaModal.valor_total}€
                    </p>
                  </div>
                </div>

                {/* BOTÃO DE PAGAMENTO EXTRA DIRETO NO MODAL */}
                {reservaModal.status_pagamento === 'Aguardando Pagamento Extra' && (Number(reservaModal.valor_total) - Number(reservaModal.valor_pago || 0) > 0) && (
                  <div className="mt-6 bg-white/10 border border-white/20 p-5 rounded-xl flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-center justify-between gap-4">
                    <div className="text-center sm:text-left w-full">
                      <p className="text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-blue-200 m-0 mb-1">Ajuste Pendente</p>
                      <p className="text-2xl font-black text-white m-0 leading-none">{(Number(reservaModal.valor_total) - Number(reservaModal.valor_pago || 0)).toFixed(2)}€</p>
                    </div>
                    <button 
                      onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); handlePagarRestante(reservaModal, Number(reservaModal.valor_total) - Number(reservaModal.valor_pago || 0)); }}
                      disabled={loadingStripe === reservaModal.id}
                      className="bg-[#e2a41d] text-white font-black text-[10px] uppercase tracking-widest px-6 py-3.5 rounded-xl hover:bg-[#d4991b] transition-colors disabled:opacity-50 w-full sm:w-auto shadow-md"
                    >
                      {loadingStripe === reservaModal.id ? 'A processar...' : 'Pagar Ajuste Agora'}
                    </button>
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>

              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-6">
                <div>
                  <h4 className="text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-slate-400 mb-3">{isEn ? 'Program Details' : 'O Programa'}</h4>
                  <div className="bg-slate-50 p-5 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 shadow-sm h-full">
                    <p className="text-lg font-black text-slate-900 mb-2 leading-tight">{isEn && reservaModal.campos?.nome_en ? reservaModal.campos?.nome_en : reservaModal.campos?.nome}</p>
                    <p className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-500 mb-5">📍 {isEn && reservaModal.campos?.local_en ? reservaModal.campos?.local_en : reservaModal.campos?.local}</p>
                    <div className="border-t border-slate-200 pt-4">
                      <p className="text-[9px] font-black text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-widest mb-1">{isEn ? 'Shift/Dates' : 'Turno Selecionado'}</p>
                      <p className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-800 leading-tight">{reservaModal.turno_nome}</p>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div>
                  <h4 className="text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-slate-400 mb-3">{isEn ? 'Participant Info' : 'O Participante'}</h4>
                  <div className="bg-white p-5 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 shadow-sm h-full">
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-3 mb-5">
                      <div className="w-12 h-12 bg-slate-100 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center text-xl shadow-inner flex-shrink-0">👦</div>
                      <div className="min-w-0">
                        <p className="text-base font-black text-slate-900 leading-tight mb-1 truncate">{reservaModal.criancas?.nome || 'N/D'}</p>
                        <p className="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-widest text-slate-500">NIF: {reservaModal.criancas?.nif || 'Não preenchido'}</p>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                    <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 text-sm border-t border-slate-100 pt-4">
                      <div>
                        <span className="block text-[9px] font-black text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-widest mb-1">{isEn ? 'Allergies' : 'Alergias'}</span>
                        <span className="font-bold text-red-700 text-xs">{reservaModal.criancas?.restricoes_alimentares || 'Nenhuma'}</span>
                      </div>
                      <div>
                        <span className="block text-[9px] font-black text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-widest mb-1">{isEn ? 'Chronic Diseases' : 'Doenças Crónicas'}</span>
                        <span className="font-bold text-red-700 text-xs">{reservaModal.criancas?.doencas_cronicas || 'Não'}</span>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* SEÇÃO PEDIDO DE ALTERAÇÃO / EXTRAS */}
              {reservaModal.status_pagamento !== 'Reembolsado' && reservaModal.status_reembolso !== 'Reembolsado' && (
                <div className="border border-slate-200 bg-white rounded-2xl p-5 md:p-6 overflow-hidden transition-all shadow-sm">
                  <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row justify-between items-start sm:items-center gap-4">
                    <div>
                      <h4 className="text-sm font-black text-slate-900 m-0">{isEn ? 'Need to add an extra or change days?' : 'Precisa de adicionar extras ou alterar os dias?'}</h4>
                      <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 font-medium m-0 mt-1">{isEn ? 'Send a request directly to the organizer.' : 'Envie um pedido ao organizador e pague a diferença de forma fácil.'}</p>
                    </div>
                    {!changeRequest.active && (
                      <button onClick={() => setChangeRequest({ ...changeRequest, active: true })} className="w-full sm:w-auto px-5 py-3 bg-slate-900 text-white text-[10px] uppercase tracking-widest font-black rounded-xl shadow-md hover:bg-[#0E3A68] transition-colors whitespace-nowrap">
                        {isEn ? 'Request Change' : 'Solicitar Alteração'}
                      </button>
                    )}
                  </div>

                  {changeRequest.active && (
                    <div className="mt-5 pt-5 border-t border-slate-100 animate-in fade-in slide-in-from-top-2">
                      <label className="block text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-slate-500 mb-2">{isEn ? 'Describe what you need:' : 'Descreva a alteração ou extras pretendidos:'}</label>
                      <textarea 
                        rows={3} 
                        placeholder={isEn ? "E.g., I would like to add lunch for all days." : "Ex: Olá, gostava de adicionar a opção de alimentação para todos os dias do meu filho."}
                        value={changeRequest.text}
                        onChange={(e) => setChangeRequest({ ...changeRequest, text: e.target.value })}
                        className="w-full p-4 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 text-sm font-medium outline-none focus:border-[#0E3A68] bg-slate-50 resize-none mb-4"
                      />
                      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row gap-3 justify-end">
                        <button onClick={() => setChangeRequest({ active: false, text: "", loading: false })} className="px-6 py-3 text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-slate-500 hover:bg-slate-100 rounded-xl transition-colors border border-transparent hover:border-slate-200 w-full sm:w-auto">Cancelar</button>
                        <button onClick={() => handleEnviarPedidoAlteracao(reservaModal)} disabled={changeRequest.loading} className="px-8 py-3 text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-white bg-[#0E3A68] rounded-xl hover:bg-[#0b2b4d] shadow-md transition-colors disabled:opacity-50 w-full sm:w-auto">
                          {changeRequest.loading ? (isEn ? 'Sending...' : 'A enviar...') : (isEn ? 'Send Request' : 'Enviar Pedido ao Organizador')}
                        </button>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  )}
                </div>
              )}
