                <p className="text-[13px] font-bold text-amber-700 m-0">{isEn ? 'Already have an account?' : 'Já tem conta criada?'}</p>
              </div>
              <button type="button" onClick={() => { sessionStorage.setItem('redirect_after_login', window.location.href); router.push(`/${lang}/login`); }} className="w-full sm:w-auto px-6 py-3.5 bg-amber-600 hover:bg-amber-700 text-white font-black uppercase tracking-widest rounded-xl text-[10px] shadow-md transition-all hover:-translate-y-0.5">
                {isEn ? 'Login to speed up' : 'Fazer Login e acelerar'}
              </button>
            </div>
          )}

          {isExterna && (
            <div className="bg-blue-50 p-6 md:p-8 rounded-[2rem] border border-blue-200 flex items-start gap-4 shadow-sm">
              <IconExternalLink className="w-8 h-8 text-blue-600 flex-shrink-0 mt-1" />
              <div>
                <h3 className="text-lg font-black text-blue-900 m-0 mb-2">{isEn ? 'External Registration' : 'Inscrição Externa Oficial'}</h3>
                <p className="text-sm font-medium text-blue-800 m-0 leading-relaxed">
                  {isEn 
                    ? 'This program uses its own registration portal. Please enter your contact details below so we can notify the partner, and we will securely redirect you to their official platform to complete the process.'
                    : 'Este programa utiliza um portal próprio de inscrições. Introduza os seus dados de contacto rápidos para notificarmos o parceiro e redirecioná-lo-emos com segurança para a plataforma oficial do organizador.'}
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="flex flex-col gap-8">

            {/* DADOS PARENTE (GUEST OU EXTERNO SEMPRE PRECISA) */}
            {(isGuest || isExterna) && (
              <section className="bg-white p-6 md:p-10 rounded-[2rem] border border-slate-200 shadow-sm">
                <h2 className="text-xl md:text-2xl font-black text-slate-900 m-0 mb-8 border-b border-slate-100 pb-4 tracking-tight">{isEn ? 'Your Contact Details' : 'Os Seus Dados de Contacto'}</h2>
                <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-6">
                  <div className="sm:col-span-2"><label className={labelClass}>{isEn ? 'Full Name' : 'Nome Completo'} *</label><input type="text" required value={guestData.nome} onChange={e => setGuestData({ ...guestData, nome: e.target.value })} className={inputClass} /></div>
                  <div><label className={labelClass}>{isEn ? 'Email Address' : 'E-mail'} *</label><input type="email" required value={guestData.email} onChange={e => setGuestData({ ...guestData, email: e.target.value })} className={inputClass} /></div>
                  <div><label className={labelClass}>{isEn ? 'Phone Number' : 'Telefone'} *</label><input type="tel" required value={guestData.telefone} onChange={e => setGuestData({ ...guestData, telefone: e.target.value })} className={inputClass} /></div>
                </div>
              </section>
            )}

            {/* PARTICIPANTES E ESCOLHAS (ESCONDIDO SE FOR EXTERNO) */}
            {!isExterna && (
              <section className="bg-white p-6 md:p-10 rounded-[2rem] border border-slate-200 shadow-sm">
                <h2 className="text-xl md:text-2xl font-black text-slate-900 m-0 mb-8 border-b border-slate-100 pb-4 tracking-tight">{isEn ? 'Participants' : 'Participantes'}</h2>

                {Array.from({ length: quantidade }).map((_, i) => {
                  const childId = selecoesCriancas[i];
                  const childInfo = criancas.find(c => c.id === childId);
                  const participantNumber = i + 1;
                  const escolhasUrlLocais = opcoesParticipantesUrl[participantNumber]; 

                  return (
                    <div key={i} className={i !== quantidade - 1 ? "mb-10 pb-10 border-b-2 border-dashed border-slate-100" : ""}>
                      <label className="block text-[11px] font-black text-[#0E3A68] mb-3 uppercase tracking-widest ml-1">{isEn ? `Participant ${participantNumber}` : `Participante ${participantNumber}`}</label>

                      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row gap-4 items-center">
                        <select required value={childId} onChange={(e) => { const novas = [...selecoesCriancas]; novas[i] = e.target.value; setSelecoesCriancas(novas); }} className={`${selectClass} flex-1`}>
                          <option value="">{isEn ? 'Choose a child...' : 'Escolha um participante...'}</option>
                          {criancas.map(c => {
                            const idade = calcularIdade(c.data_nascimento);
                            const idadeForaLimite = (campo.idade_min && idade < campo.idade_min) || (campo.idade_max && idade > campo.idade_max);
                            const jaInscrito = reservasExistentes.some(r => r.crianca_id === c.id && r.turno_nome === (turnoSelecionado?.nome || 'Programa Base'));
                            const jaSelec = selecoesCriancas.includes(c.id) && selecoesCriancas[i] !== c.id;
                            const isDisabled = idadeForaLimite || jaInscrito || jaSelec;
                            
                            let aviso = "";
                            if (idadeForaLimite) aviso = isEn ? `(Age not allowed: ${idade} yrs)` : `(Idade não permitida: ${idade} anos)`;
                            else if (jaInscrito) aviso = isEn ? "(Already registered)" : "(Já inscrito neste turno)";
                            else if (jaSelec) aviso = isEn ? "(Selected in this order)" : "(Selecionado nesta compra)";

                            return <option key={c.id} value={c.id} disabled={isDisabled}>{c.nome} {aviso}</option>;
                          })}
                        </select>
                        <button type="button" onClick={() => openNewChildModal(i)} className="w-full sm:w-auto px-6 py-4 bg-blue-50 hover:bg-blue-100 text-[#0E3A68] font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-[10px] rounded-xl border border-blue-200 transition-colors shadow-sm">+ {isEn ? 'New' : 'Novo'}</button>
                      </div>

                      {childInfo && (
                        <div className="mt-6 bg-slate-50 p-6 md:p-8 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 shadow-inner">
                          <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-6"><h4 className="text-[10px] font-black text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-widest m-0">{isEn ? 'Verify Details' : 'Confirmar Identificação'}</h4></div>
                          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-6">
                            <div className="sm:col-span-2"><label className={labelClass}>{isEn ? 'Full Name' : 'Nome Completo'}</label><input type="text" required value={childInfo.nome || ''} onChange={e => handleUpdateLocalCrianca(childId, 'nome', e.target.value)} onBlur={e => handleSaveDBCrianca(childId, 'nome', e.target.value)} className={inputClass} /></div>
                            <div><label className={labelClass}>{isEn ? 'Date of Birth' : 'Data Nascimento'}</label><input type="date" required value={childInfo.data_nascimento || ''} onChange={e => handleUpdateLocalCrianca(childId, 'data_nascimento', e.target.value)} onBlur={e => handleSaveDBCrianca(childId, 'data_nascimento', e.target.value)} className={inputClass} /></div>
                            <div><label className={labelClass}>{isEn ? 'Gender' : 'Sexo'}</label>
                              <select value={childInfo.sexo || ''} onChange={e => { handleUpdateLocalCrianca(childId, 'sexo', e.target.value); handleSaveDBCrianca(childId, 'sexo', e.target.value); }} className={selectClass}>
                                <option value="Masculino">Masculino</option><option value="Feminino">Feminino</option><option value="Prefiro não dizer">Prefiro não dizer</option>
                              </select>
                            </div>

                            <div className="sm:col-span-2 border-t border-slate-200 pt-6 mt-2"><label className={`${labelClass} !text-red-700`}>{isEn ? 'Medical Alerts' : 'Alertas Clínicos'}</label></div>
                            <div className="sm:col-span-2"><label className={`${labelClass} !text-red-700`}>{isEn ? 'Allergies / Restrictions' : 'Alergias Alimentares'}</label><input type="text" value={childInfo.restricoes_alimentares || ''} onChange={e => handleUpdateLocalCrianca(childId, 'restricoes_alimentares', e.target.value)} onBlur={e => handleSaveDBCrianca(childId, 'restricoes_alimentares', e.target.value)} className={`${inputClass} !border-red-200 focus:!border-red-500`} placeholder={isEn ? "None" : "Nenhuma"} /></div>
                            <div className="sm:col-span-2"><label className={`${labelClass} !text-red-700`}>{isEn ? 'Chronic Diseases' : 'Doenças Crónicas ou Medicação'}</label><input type="text" value={childInfo.doencas_cronicas || ''} onChange={e => handleUpdateLocalCrianca(childId, 'doencas_cronicas', e.target.value)} onBlur={e => handleSaveDBCrianca(childId, 'doencas_cronicas', e.target.value)} className={`${inputClass} !border-red-200 focus:!border-red-500`} placeholder={isEn ? "None" : "Nenhuma"} /></div>
                          </div>
                        </div>
                      )}

                      {/* OPÇÕES MÚLTIPLAS */}
                      {childInfo && escolhasUrlLocais && Object.keys(escolhasUrlLocais).length > 0 && (
                        <div className="mt-4 bg-emerald-50 p-6 rounded-2xl border border-emerald-200 shadow-sm flex flex-col gap-3">
                          <h4 className="text-[10px] font-black text-emerald-800 uppercase tracking-widest m-0">✓ Opções de Programa</h4>
                          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4 mt-2">
                            {Object.entries(escolhasUrlLocais).map(([perg, resp]: any) => (
                              <div key={perg} className="bg-white p-4 border border-emerald-100 rounded-xl shadow-sm">
                                <p className="text-[9px] font-bold uppercase tracking-widest text-slate-400 m-0 mb-1">{perg}</p>
                                <p className="text-sm font-black text-emerald-700 m-0">{resp}</p>
                              </div>
                            ))}
                          </div>
                        </div>
                      )}

                      {/* PERGUNTAS TEXTO LIVRE */}
                      {childInfo && perguntasDeTexto.length > 0 && (
                        <div className="mt-4 bg-blue-50 p-6 md:p-8 rounded-2xl border border-blue-200 shadow-sm">
                          <h4 className="text-[10px] font-black text-[#0E3A68] uppercase tracking-widest mb-5">{isEn ? 'Specific Questions' : 'Perguntas do Organizador'}</h4>
                          <div className="grid gap-6">
                            {perguntasDeTexto.map((pOriginal: string, pIdx: number) => {
                              const pTituloPt = pOriginal.startsWith('TEXTO|') ? pOriginal.split('|')[1] : pOriginal;
                              const originalIndex = campo.perguntas_customizadas.indexOf(pOriginal);
                              const pEnRaw = campo.perguntas_customizadas_en?.[originalIndex];
                              const pTituloEn = pEnRaw ? (pEnRaw.startsWith('TEXTO|') ? pEnRaw.split('|')[1] : pEnRaw) : pTituloPt;
                              const pVisivel = isEn ? pTituloEn : pTituloPt;

                              return (
                                <div key={pIdx}>
                                  <label className={`${labelClass} !text-[#0E3A68] mb-2 block`}>{pVisivel} *</label>
                                  <input
                                    type="text" required 
                                    value={respostasCustomizadas[i]?.[pTituloPt] || ''}
                                    onChange={e => setRespostasCustomizadas(prev => ({...prev, [i]: {...(prev[i]||{}), [pTituloPt]: e.target.value}}))}
                                    className={`${inputClass} !border-blue-200 focus:!border-[#0E3A68]`} 
                                    placeholder={isEn ? 'Your answer...' : 'A sua resposta...'}
                                  />
                                </div>
                              );
                            })}
                          </div>
                        </div>
                      )}
                    </div>
                  );
                })}
              </section>
            )}

            {/* PAGAMENTO E TERMOS (STRIPE OU EXTERNO) */}
            <section className="bg-white p-6 md:p-10 rounded-[2rem] border border-slate-200 shadow-sm">
              
              {!isExterna ? (
                <>
                  <h2 className="text-xl md:text-2xl font-black text-slate-900 m-0 mb-6 border-b border-slate-100 pb-4 tracking-tight">{isEn ? 'Secure Payment' : 'Pagamento Seguro'}</h2>

                  {isPagamentoFracionado ? (
                    <div className="p-6 md:p-8 bg-blue-50 border border-blue-200 rounded-2xl mb-6 shadow-inner">
                      <p className="font-black text-[#0E3A68] mb-2 m-0 text-base">{isEn ? 'Payment Plan (50% Deposit)' : 'Facilidade de Pagamento (Sinal de 50%)'}</p>
                      <p className="text-sm font-medium text-blue-800 m-0 leading-relaxed">{isEn ? `Pay only ${valorACobrarAgora.toFixed(2)}€ today. The balance is charged 1 week prior.` : `Garanta a vaga pagando apenas um sinal de ${valorACobrarAgora.toFixed(2)}€. O remanescente (${valorPendente.toFixed(2)}€) é debitado automaticamente 1 semana antes do programa.`}</p>
                    </div>
                  ) : (
                    <div className="p-6 md:p-8 bg-[#059669]/10 border border-[#059669]/20 rounded-2xl mb-6 shadow-inner">
                      <p className="font-black text-[#059669] mb-2 m-0 text-base">{isEn ? 'Full Payment' : 'Pagamento a Pronto (100%)'}</p>
                      <p className="text-sm font-medium text-[#059669]/80 m-0 leading-relaxed">{isEn ? `Full payment of ${valorACobrarAgora.toFixed(2)}€ is required today.` : `O campo exige o pagamento total (${valorACobrarAgora.toFixed(2)}€) para garantir e bloquear a vaga no sistema imediatamente.`}</p>
                    </div>
                  )}

                  <div className="p-6 bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-2xl flex items-center justify-between flex-wrap gap-4 mb-8">
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
                      <div className="w-12 h-12 rounded-xl bg-slate-900 text-white flex items-center justify-center shadow-md flex-shrink-0">
                        <IconCreditCard className="w-6 h-6" />
                      </div>
                      <div>
                        <p className="font-black text-slate-900 m-0 mb-1">Stripe Checkout</p>
                        <p className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-500 m-0">
                          {isEn ? 'Pay securely via Card.' : 'MB WAY, Cartão de Crédito ou Débito.'}
                        </p>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                    <div className="flex gap-3 items-center font-black text-slate-900 text-sm opacity-80">
                      <span className="italic text-[#0E3A68]">MB WAY</span> 
                      <span>VISA</span> 
                      <span>MASTERCARD</span>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </>
              ) : (
                <h2 className="text-xl md:text-2xl font-black text-slate-900 m-0 mb-6 border-b border-slate-100 pb-4 tracking-tight">{isEn ? 'Terms & Redirection' : 'Termos & Redirecionamento'}</h2>
              )}

              <div className="p-6 bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-2xl">
                <label className="flex items-start gap-4 cursor-pointer group">
                  <input type="checkbox" required checked={concordaTermos} onChange={e => setConcordaTermos(e.target.checked)} className="mt-1 w-6 h-6 accent-[#0E3A68] cursor-pointer flex-shrink-0" />
                  <span className="text-xs md:text-sm font-bold text-slate-600 leading-relaxed group-hover:text-slate-900 transition-colors">
                    {isEn ? (
                      <>I declare that I am of legal age and accept HelloCamp's <Link className="text-[#0E3A68] font-black underline" href={`/${lang}/termos`} target="_blank">Terms & Conditions</Link>. {isGuest && !isExterna && " A secure account will be generated for you."}</>
                    ) : (
                      <>Declaro que sou maior de idade e aceito os <Link className="text-[#0E3A68] font-black underline" href={`/${lang}/termos`} target="_blank">Termos e Condições</Link> da plataforma. {isGuest && !isExterna && "Ao prosseguir, será gerada uma conta segura associada ao seu email."}</>
                    )}
                  </span>
                </label>
              </div>
            </section>

            {/* BOTÃO DE CONFIRMAÇÃO */}
            <div className="pt-4">
              <button type="submit" disabled={processingState || !concordaTermos} className={`w-full py-5 px-6 text-sm uppercase tracking-widest font-black rounded-2xl border-none transition-all flex items-center justify-center gap-3 ${processingState || !concordaTermos ? 'bg-slate-200 text-slate-400 cursor-not-allowed' : (isExterna ? 'bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-700 text-white shadow-xl shadow-blue-600/30 hover:-translate-y-1' : 'bg-[#e2a41d] hover:bg-[#d4991b] text-white shadow-xl shadow-[#e2a41d]/30 hover:-translate-y-1')} cursor-pointer`}>
                {isExterna ? (
                  <>
                    {isEn ? 'Proceed to External Booking' : 'Avançar para Inscrição Externa'}
                    <IconExternalLink className="w-5 h-5" />
                  </>
                ) : (
                  isEn ? `Pay ${valorACobrarAgora.toFixed(2)}€` : `Confirmar e Pagar ${valorACobrarAgora.toFixed(2)}€`
                )}
              </button>
            </div>
          </form>
        </div>

        {/* ========================================== */}
        {/* SIDEBAR DIREITA - RESUMO DE COMPRA         */}
        {/* ========================================== */}
        <aside className="flex-[1_1_35%] min-w-[320px] w-full lg:sticky lg:top-8">
          <div className="bg-white p-6 md:p-8 rounded-[2rem] shadow-sm border border-slate-200">
            <h3 className="text-lg font-black mb-6 border-b border-slate-100 pb-5 uppercase tracking-widest text-slate-900">{isEn ? 'Booking Summary' : 'Resumo de Compra'}</h3>

            <div className="flex flex-col gap-6 mb-8">
              <div>
                <span className="block text-[10px] font-black text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-widest mb-1.5">{isEn ? 'Program' : 'Programa'}</span>
                <p className="text-sm font-black text-slate-900 m-0 leading-tight">{turnoSelecionado?.nome || campo?.nome}</p>
              </div>

              {turnoSelecionado?.dias_soltos?.length > 0 && (
                <div>
                  <span className="block text-[10px] font-black text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-widest mb-2">{isEn ? 'Selected Dates' : 'Datas Selecionadas'}</span>
                  <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
                    {turnoSelecionado.dias_soltos.map((d: string) => (
                      <span key={d} className="bg-slate-100 border border-slate-200 text-slate-700 text-[10px] font-bold px-3 py-1.5 rounded-md shadow-sm">
                        {new Date(d).toLocaleDateString(isEn ? 'en-GB' : 'pt-PT', { day: '2-digit', month: 'short' })}
                      </span>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                </div>
              )}

              <div className="flex justify-between items-center border-b border-slate-100 pb-5">
                <span className="text-[10px] font-black text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-widest">{isEn ? `Base Price (x${quantidade})` : `Preço Base (x${quantidade})`}</span>
                <span className="text-base font-black text-slate-900">{(precoBaseUnitario * quantidade).toFixed(2)}€</span>
              </div>

              {(extSeguro || extTransporte) && !isExterna && (
                <div className="border-b border-slate-100 pb-5">
                  <span className="block text-[10px] font-black text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-widest mb-3">{isEn ? 'Extras' : 'Suplementos Extras'}</span>
                  <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
                    {extSeguro && <div className="flex justify-between items-center"><span className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-600 flex items-center gap-2">🛡️ {isEn ? 'Insurance' : 'Seguro'}</span><span className="font-black text-slate-900">{(custoSeguroUnit * quantidade).toFixed(2)}€</span></div>}
                    {extTransporte && <div className="flex justify-between items-center"><span className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-600 flex items-center gap-2">🚌 {isEn ? 'Transport' : 'Transporte'}</span><span className="font-black text-slate-900">{(custoTransporteUnit * quantidade).toFixed(2)}€</span></div>}
                  </div>
                </div>
              )}

              {/* TAG INFORMATIVA DE OPÇÕES EXTRA */}
              {(Object.keys(opcoesParticipantesUrl).length > 0 || Object.keys(respostasCustomizadas).length > 0) && !isExterna && (
                <div className="mt-2 text-[9px] font-bold text-emerald-600 uppercase tracking-widest text-right">
                  ✓ Personalização Confirmada
                </div>
              )}
            </div>

            <div className={`p-6 rounded-2xl mb-4 flex justify-between items-center shadow-lg ${isExterna ? 'bg-blue-600 shadow-blue-600/20' : 'bg-[#0E3A68] shadow-[#0E3A68]/20'}`}>
              <span className="text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-white/80">{isEn ? 'Estimated Total' : 'Total Estimado'}</span>
              <span className="text-3xl font-black text-white">{precoFinalTotal.toFixed(2)}€</span>
            </div>

            {!isExterna ? (
              <>
                <div className="flex justify-between items-center bg-[#059669]/10 border border-[#059669]/20 p-5 rounded-2xl shadow-sm">
                  <span className="text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-[#059669]">{isEn ? 'To Pay Now' : 'A Pagar Agora'}</span>
                  <span className="text-2xl font-black text-[#059669]">{valorACobrarAgora.toFixed(2)}€</span>
                </div>

                {isPagamentoFracionado && (
                  <div className="flex justify-between items-center mt-4 p-2">
                    <span className="text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-slate-400">{isEn ? 'Pending Balance' : 'Faltará Pagar'}</span>
                    <span className="text-lg font-black text-[#e2a41d]">{valorPendente.toFixed(2)}€</span>
                  </div>
                )}
                
                <div className="flex items-center justify-center gap-2 mt-8 text-[10px] font-black text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-widest">
                  <IconShield className="w-3.5 h-3.5"/> {isEn ? '100% Secure Payment' : 'Pagamento 100% Seguro'}
                </div>
              </>
            ) : (
              <div className="text-center mt-6">
                <p className="text-[10px] font-black text-blue-500 uppercase tracking-widest leading-relaxed">
                  {isEn ? 'Payment handled externally by the organizer.' : 'Gestão e pagamento efetuados na plataforma oficial do parceiro.'}
                </p>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        </aside>

      </div>
    </main>
  );
}
</file>

"use client";

import dynamic from "next/dynamic";

const MapaMapLibre = dynamic(() => import("./MapaMapLibre"), {

<file path="app/[lang]/(public)/pesquisa/pais/[paisNome]/page.tsx">
import { Metadata } from "next";
import { supabase } from "@/lib/supabase";
import Link from "next/link";
import BotaoFavorito from "../../../../(public)/components/BotaoFavorito";
import { unstable_cache } from "next/cache";

export const dynamic = 'force-dynamic';

const escapeOr = (str: string) => str.replace(/[,()]/g, ' ').trim();

// ==========================================
// CACHE DE DADOS (24 HORAS) - ZERO EGRESS
// ==========================================
const getPaisCache = unstable_cache(
  async (nomePais: string) => {
    const { data } = await supabase.from('paises').select('*').ilike('nome', nomePais).single();
    return data;
  },
  ['cache-pais-detalhe'],
  { revalidate: 86400 }
);

const getCamposPorPaisCache = unstable_cache(
  async (nomePais: string) => {
    const { data } = await supabase
      .from("campos")
      .select("*")
      .not('contrato_parceiro_url', 'is', null)
      .or(`pais.ilike.%${nomePais}%,pais_en.ilike.%${nomePais}%`)
      .order('id', { ascending: false });
    return data || [];
  },
  ['cache-campos-pais'],
  { revalidate: 86400 }
);

// ==========================================
// 1. SEO METADATA (Com Tradução Segura)
// ==========================================
export async function generateMetadata({
  params
}: {
  params: Promise<{ lang: string; paisNome: string }>
}): Promise<Metadata> {
  const { lang, paisNome } = await params;
  const isEn = lang === 'en';
  const nomeLimpo = decodeURIComponent(paisNome).replace(/[%_]/g, '');

  const pais = await getPaisCache(nomeLimpo);

  // Fallback de Tradução Seguro
  const fallbackEN: Record<string, string> = { "Reino Unido": "United Kingdom", "Espanha": "Spain", "França": "France", "Estados Unidos": "United States", "Portugal": "Portugal" };
  const nomeIngles = fallbackEN[nomeLimpo] || nomeLimpo;
  const nomeFinalApresentar = isEn ? (pais?.nome_en || nomeIngles) : (pais?.nome || nomeLimpo);

  if (!pais) {
    return { title: isEn ? `Camps in ${nomeFinalApresentar} | HelloCamp` : `Campos em ${nomeFinalApresentar} | HelloCamp` };
  }

  const title = isEn
    ? (pais.seo_titulo_en || `Summer Camps in ${nomeFinalApresentar} | HelloCamp`)
    : (pais.seo_titulo || `Campos de Férias em ${nomeFinalApresentar} | HelloCamp`);

  const description = isEn ? pais.seo_descricao_en : pais.seo_descricao;

  return {
    title: title,
    description: description,
    openGraph: {
      title: title,
      description: description || '',
    }
  };
}

// ==========================================
// 2. COMPONENTE PRINCIPAL
// ==========================================
export default async function PesquisaPorPais({
  params,
  searchParams
}: {
  params: Promise<{ lang: string; paisNome: string }>;
  searchParams?: Promise<{ [key: string]: string | undefined }>
}) {
  const { lang, paisNome } = await params;
  const sp = await searchParams;
  const isEn = lang === 'en';

  const nomePaisInicial = decodeURIComponent(paisNome).replace(/[%_]/g, '');

  const categoriaParam = sp?.categoria || "";
  const distritoParam = sp?.distrito || "";
  const idadeParam = sp?.idade || "";
  const dataParam = sp?.data || "";

  const mostrarDistritos = nomePaisInicial.toLowerCase() === "portugal";

  const dadosPais = await getPaisCache(nomePaisInicial);
  const todosOsCamposDoPais = await getCamposPorPaisCache(nomePaisInicial);

  let resultados = todosOsCamposDoPais;

  if (categoriaParam) {
    resultados = resultados.filter(c => c.categoria === categoriaParam);
  }
  if (distritoParam && mostrarDistritos) {
    resultados = resultados.filter(c => (c.Distrito || '').toLowerCase().includes(escapeOr(distritoParam).toLowerCase()));
  }
  if (idadeParam) {
    resultados = resultados.filter(c => c.idade === idadeParam);
  }
  if (dataParam) {
    resultados = resultados.filter(c => {
      if (!c.data_inicio) return false;
      return new Date(c.data_inicio) >= new Date(dataParam);
    });
  }

  // Fallback de Tradução Seguro para a Interface
  const fallbackEN: Record<string, string> = { "Reino Unido": "United Kingdom", "Espanha": "Spain", "França": "France", "Estados Unidos": "United States", "Portugal": "Portugal" };
  const nomeIngles = fallbackEN[nomePaisInicial] || nomePaisInicial;
  const nomePaisApresentar = isEn ? (dadosPais?.nome_en || nomeIngles) : (dadosPais?.nome || nomePaisInicial);

  const descricaoPadrao = isEn
    ? `Explore premium holiday camps in ${nomePaisApresentar}. From thrilling outdoor adventures and intensive sports clubs to creative arts and language classes, discover handpicked programs.`
    : `Explore os melhores campos de férias em ${nomePaisApresentar}. Desde aventuras ao ar livre e atividades desportivas até programas de artes e línguas, descubra opções premium desenhadas para experiências inesquecíveis.`;

  const descPaisFinal = isEn
    ? (dadosPais?.seo_descricao_en || descricaoPadrao)
    : (dadosPais?.seo_descricao || descricaoPadrao);

  const distritosPT = ["Aveiro", "Beja", "Braga", "Bragança", "Castelo Branco", "Coimbra", "Évora", "Faro", "Guarda", "Leiria", "Lisboa", "Portalegre", "Porto", "Santarém", "Setúbal", "Viana do Castelo", "Vila Real", "Viseu"];

  const baseUrl = "https://www.hellocamp.pt";
  const breadcrumbSchema = {
    "@context": "https://schema.org",
    "@type": "BreadcrumbList",
    "itemListElement": [
      { "@type": "ListItem", "position": 1, "name": isEn ? "Home" : "Início", "item": `${baseUrl}/${lang}` },
      { "@type": "ListItem", "position": 2, "name": isEn ? "Countries" : "Países", "item": `${baseUrl}/${lang}/pesquisa` },
      { "@type": "ListItem", "position": 3, "name": nomePaisApresentar, "item": `${baseUrl}/${lang}/pesquisa/pais/${encodeURIComponent(nomePaisInicial)}` }
    ]
  };

  return (
    <main className="min-h-screen bg-slate-50 text-slate-900 font-sans pb-20">
      <script type="application/ld+json" dangerouslySetInnerHTML={{ __html: JSON.stringify(breadcrumbSchema) }} />

      <section className="bg-white pt-10 pb-8 px-4 md:px-6 border-b border-slate-200 shadow-sm relative z-20">
        <div className="max-w-[1100px] mx-auto">
          <nav className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs font-black text-slate-400 mb-6 tracking-widest uppercase">
            <Link href={`/${lang}`} className="hover:text-slate-900 transition-colors">{isEn ? 'Home' : 'Início'}</Link>
            <span>/</span>
            <Link href={`/${lang}/pesquisa`} className="hover:text-slate-900 transition-colors">{isEn ? 'Countries' : 'Países'}</Link>
            <span>/</span>
            <span className="text-slate-900">{nomePaisApresentar}</span>
          </nav>

          <div className="max-w-3xl">
            <span className="block text-[10px] font-black text-slate-500 uppercase tracking-widest mb-3 bg-slate-100 w-max px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-200">
              {isEn ? 'Destination Guide' : 'Guia de Destino'}
            </span>
            <h1 className="text-4xl md:text-5xl font-black text-slate-900 mb-4 tracking-tight capitalize">
              {isEn ? `Camps in ${nomePaisApresentar}` : `Campos em ${nomePaisApresentar}`}
            </h1>
            <p className="text-base md:text-lg text-slate-500 leading-relaxed font-medium">
              {descPaisFinal}
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      <section className="bg-white border-b border-slate-200 py-4 px-4 md:px-6 sticky top-[72px] md:top-[80px] z-30 shadow-[0_8px_30px_rgb(0,0,0,0.04)]">
        <div className="max-w-[1100px] mx-auto">
          <form action={`/${lang}/pesquisa/pais/${encodeURIComponent(nomePaisInicial)}`} method="GET" className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-4 lg:grid-cols-5 gap-3 items-center">

            <select name="categoria" defaultValue={categoriaParam} className="w-full p-3 bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl text-sm font-bold text-slate-700 outline-none cursor-pointer focus:border-slate-900 focus:bg-white transition-colors">
              <option value="">{isEn ? 'All Categories' : 'Todas as Categorias'}</option>
              <option value="Desporto">{isEn ? 'Sports' : 'Desporto'}</option>
              <option value="Aventura & Natureza">{isEn ? 'Adventure' : 'Aventura & Natureza'}</option>
              <option value="Tecnologia & Ciência">{isEn ? 'Tech' : 'Tecnologia & Ciência'}</option>
              <option value="Artes & Criatividade">{isEn ? 'Arts' : 'Artes & Criatividade'}</option>
              <option value="Línguas">{isEn ? 'Languages' : 'Línguas'}</option>
            </select>

            {mostrarDistritos && (
              <select name="distrito" defaultValue={distritoParam} className="w-full p-3 bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl text-sm font-bold text-slate-700 outline-none cursor-pointer focus:border-slate-900 focus:bg-white transition-colors">
                <option value="">{isEn ? 'All Districts' : 'Todos os Distritos'}</option>
                {distritosPT.map(d => <option key={d} value={d}>{d}</option>)}
              </select>
            )}

            <select name="idade" defaultValue={idadeParam} className="w-full p-3 bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl text-sm font-bold text-slate-700 outline-none cursor-pointer focus:border-slate-900 focus:bg-white transition-colors">
              <option value="">{isEn ? 'All Ages' : 'Todas as Idades'}</option>
              <option value="6-9 anos">6-9 {isEn ? 'years' : 'anos'}</option>
              <option value="10-13 anos">10-13 {isEn ? 'years' : 'anos'}</option>
              <option value="14-17 anos">14-17 {isEn ? 'years' : 'anos'}</option>
            </select>

            <input
              type="date"
              name="data"
              defaultValue={dataParam}
              min={new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0]}
              className="w-full p-3 bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl text-sm font-bold text-slate-700 outline-none cursor-pointer focus:border-slate-900 focus:bg-white transition-colors"
            />

            <button type="submit" className="w-full p-3 bg-[#e2a41d] hover:bg-[#d4991b] text-white rounded-xl text-sm font-black transition-colors shadow-sm uppercase tracking-widest">
              {isEn ? 'Update' : 'Atualizar'}
            </button>

            {(categoriaParam || distritoParam || idadeParam || dataParam) && (
              <Link href={`/${lang}/pesquisa/pais/${encodeURIComponent(nomePaisInicial)}`} className="text-xs font-bold text-red-500 hover:text-red-600 text-center sm:text-left transition-colors lg:col-span-5 uppercase tracking-widest bg-red-50 w-max px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg border border-red-100">
                ✕ {isEn ? 'Clear filters' : 'Limpar filtros'}
              </Link>
            )}
          </form>
        </div>
      </section>

      <section className="max-w-[1100px] mx-auto px-4 py-12 md:py-16">
        {!resultados || resultados.length === 0 ? (
          <div className="text-center p-12 bg-white rounded-[2rem] border border-slate-200 shadow-sm max-w-2xl mx-auto flex flex-col items-center gap-4">
            <div className="w-20 h-20 bg-slate-50 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center text-4xl border border-slate-100 mb-2">🏕️</div>
            <h3 className="text-2xl font-black text-slate-900">{isEn ? 'No camps found' : 'Nenhum programa encontrado'}</h3>
            <p className="text-base text-slate-500 font-medium">
              {isEn ? 'Try adjusting your filters or dates.' : 'Não encontrámos resultados exatos. Tente remover o filtro da data ou pesquisar noutra categoria.'}
            </p>
            <Link href={`/${lang}/pesquisa/pais/${encodeURIComponent(nomePaisInicial)}`} className="mt-4 bg-slate-900 text-white font-bold px-6 py-3 rounded-xl hover:bg-slate-800 transition-colors shadow-lg">
              {isEn ? 'Clear all filters' : 'Ver todos os programas'}
            </Link>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <>
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-8">
              <span className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-500 uppercase tracking-widest bg-white border border-slate-200 px-4 py-2 rounded-xl shadow-sm">
                {resultados.length} {isEn ? 'experiences found' : 'experiências encontradas'}
              </span>
            </div>
            
            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-3 gap-8">
              {resultados.map((campo) => {
                const nomeVisivel = isEn && campo.nome_en ? campo.nome_en : campo.nome;
                const localVisivel = isEn && campo.local_en ? campo.local_en : (campo.Distrito || campo.local);

                let precoVisivel = campo.preco || 0;
                if (!precoVisivel && campo.pacotes && campo.pacotes.length > 0) {
                  const todosPrecos = campo.pacotes.flatMap((p: any) =>
                    p.variantes ? p.variantes.map((v: any) => v.preco) : []
                  );
                  if (todosPrecos.length > 0) precoVisivel = Math.min(...todosPrecos);
                }

                return (
                  <div key={campo.id} className="group flex flex-col bg-white rounded-[2rem] overflow-hidden border border-slate-200 relative shadow-sm hover:shadow-[0_20px_60px_rgb(0,0,0,0.08)] transition-all duration-300 hover:-translate-y-1">
                    <Link href={`/${lang}/campo/${campo.id}`} className="absolute inset-0 z-10"><span className="sr-only">Explorar {nomeVisivel}</span></Link>
                    <div className="absolute top-4 right-4 z-20"><BotaoFavorito campoId={campo.id} /></div>

                    <div className="relative h-60 w-full overflow-hidden bg-slate-100">
                      <img 
                        src={campo.imagem || '/og-image.jpg'} 
                        alt={nomeVisivel} 
                        loading="lazy" 
                        className="absolute inset-0 w-full h-full object-cover transition-transform duration-700 group-hover:scale-105" 
                      />
                      <div className="absolute top-4 left-4 bg-white/90 backdrop-blur-sm px-3 py-1.5 text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-slate-900 rounded-lg z-0 shadow-sm">
                        {campo.categoria}
                      </div>
                    </div>

                    <div className="flex flex-col p-6 flex-1 pointer-events-none">
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-bold text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-widest mb-3">
                        <svg className="w-3 h-3" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="3"><path d="M21 10c0 7-9 13-9 13s-9-6-9-13a9 9 0 0 1 18 0z"></path><circle cx="12" cy="10" r="3"></circle></svg>
                        {localVisivel}
                      </div>
                      <h3 className="text-xl font-black text-slate-900 leading-tight mb-2 line-clamp-2">{nomeVisivel}</h3>
                      <p className="text-sm text-slate-500 font-medium mb-6">
                        {isEn ? 'Ages:' : 'Idades:'} <span className="text-slate-900 font-bold">{campo.idade}</span>
                      </p>

                      <div className="mt-auto pt-6 flex items-center justify-between border-t border-slate-100">
                        <div>
                          <p className="text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-slate-400 mb-0.5">{isEn ? 'From' : 'A partir de'}</p>
                          <p className="text-2xl font-black text-slate-900 m-0">{precoVisivel > 0 ? `${precoVisivel}€` : '--'}</p>
                        </div>
                        <span className="bg-slate-100 text-slate-900 px-5 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-widest transition-colors group-hover:bg-[#e2a41d] group-hover:text-white shadow-sm">
                          {isEn ? 'View Dates' : 'Ver Datas'}
                        </span>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          </>
        )}
      </section>

      <section className="bg-white border-t border-slate-200 py-20 px-4 md:px-6">
        <div className="max-w-[1100px] mx-auto text-center md:text-left">
          <h3 className="text-2xl font-black text-slate-900 mb-8">{isEn ? `Explore more in ${nomePaisApresentar}` : `Explore mais em ${nomePaisApresentar}`}</h3>
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-3 gap-6">
            <Link href={`/${lang}/pesquisa/pais/${encodeURIComponent(nomePaisInicial)}?categoria=Desporto`} className="p-6 bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-2xl hover:border-slate-900 transition-colors text-base font-black text-slate-800 no-underline flex items-center justify-between group shadow-sm hover:shadow-lg">
              <span className="flex items-center gap-3">⚽ {isEn ? `Sports Camps` : `Campos de Desporto`}</span>
              <span className="text-slate-400 group-hover:text-slate-900 transition-colors">&rarr;</span>
            </Link>
            <Link href={`/${lang}/pesquisa/pais/${encodeURIComponent(nomePaisInicial)}?categoria=Línguas`} className="p-6 bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-2xl hover:border-slate-900 transition-colors text-base font-black text-slate-800 no-underline flex items-center justify-between group shadow-sm hover:shadow-lg">
              <span className="flex items-center gap-3">🗣️ {isEn ? `Language Camps` : `Campos de Línguas`}</span>
              <span className="text-slate-400 group-hover:text-slate-900 transition-colors">&rarr;</span>
            </Link>
            <Link href={`/${lang}/pesquisa/pais/${encodeURIComponent(nomePaisInicial)}?categoria=Aventura & Natureza`} className="p-6 bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-2xl hover:border-slate-900 transition-colors text-base font-black text-slate-800 no-underline flex items-center justify-between group shadow-sm hover:shadow-lg">
              <span className="flex items-center gap-3">🏕️ {isEn ? `Adventure Camps` : `Campos de Aventura`}</span>
              <span className="text-slate-400 group-hover:text-slate-900 transition-colors">&rarr;</span>
            </Link>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>
    </main>
  );
}
</file>

<file path="app/[lang]/(public)/mapa/page.tsx">
import { supabase } from "@/lib/supabase";
import Link from "next/link";
import MapaWrapper from "./MapaWrapper";
import { getDictionary } from "@/lib/getDictionary";
import { unstable_cache } from "next/cache";

export const dynamic = 'force-dynamic';

export const metadata = {
  title: "Mapa de Campos de Férias | HelloCamp",
  description: "Explore no mapa interativo todos os campos de férias em Portugal e no Mundo. Filtre por categoria e idade.",
};

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════
// CACHE DE DADOS (24 HORAS) - ZERO EGRESS
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════
const getCamposMapaCache = unstable_cache(
  async () => {
    const { data } = await supabase
      .from("campos")
      .select("id, nome, nome_en, categoria, categoria_en, idade, idade_en, linguas_faladas, preco, imagem, latitude, longitude")
      .eq("ativo", true)
      .not("contrato_parceiro_url", "is", null);
    return data || [];
  },
  ['cache-mapa-campos-v2'],
  { revalidate: 86400 } // 24h
);

export default async function PaginaMapa({
  params,
  searchParams,
}: {
  params: Promise<{ lang: string }>;
  searchParams?: Promise<{ [key: string]: string | undefined }>;
}) {
  const { lang } = await params;
  const dict = await getDictionary(lang as "pt" | "en");
  const sp = await searchParams;
  const isEn = lang === "en";

  const categoria = sp?.categoria || "";
  const idade = sp?.idade || "";
  const lingua = sp?.lingua || "";

  // 1. CARREGAR DADOS DA CACHE (Ultra-Rápido)
  const todosOsCampos = await getCamposMapaCache();

  const categoriasMap = new Map<string, string>();
  const idadesMap = new Map<string, string>();
  const linguasMap = new Set<string>();

  todosOsCampos.forEach((c: any) => {
    if (c.categoria?.trim()) categoriasMap.set(c.categoria.trim(), c.categoria_en?.trim() || c.categoria.trim());
    if (c.idade?.trim()) idadesMap.set(c.idade.trim(), c.idade_en?.trim() || c.idade.trim());
    if (c.linguas_faladas?.trim()) linguasMap.add(c.linguas_faladas.trim());
  });

  const categoriasOpcoes = Array.from(categoriasMap.entries()).map(([pt, en]) => ({ valor: pt, label: isEn ? en : pt }));
  const idadesOpcoes = Array.from(idadesMap.entries()).map(([pt, en]) => ({ valor: pt, label: isEn ? en : pt }));
  const linguasUnicas = Array.from(linguasMap).sort();

  // 2. FILTRAGEM INSTANTÂNEA NA RAM
  let camposExibidos = todosOsCampos;

  if (categoria) camposExibidos = camposExibidos.filter(c => c.categoria === categoria);
  if (idade) camposExibidos = camposExibidos.filter(c => c.idade === idade);
  if (lingua) camposExibidos = camposExibidos.filter(c => (c.linguas_faladas || '').includes(lingua));

  const temFiltrosAtivos = !!(categoria || idade || lingua);

  return (
    <main className="flex flex-col md:flex-row h-[calc(100vh-64px)] bg-slate-50 font-sans overflow-hidden">

      {/* BARRA LATERAL (SIDEBAR DE FILTROS) */}
      <aside className="w-full md:w-[380px] bg-white border-b md:border-b-0 md:border-r border-slate-200 overflow-y-auto p-6 md:p-8 flex flex-col flex-shrink-0 shadow-[4px_0_24px_rgba(0,0,0,0.02)] z-10 max-h-[45vh] md:max-h-full">

        <Link
          href={`/${lang}`}
          className="hidden md:inline-flex items-center gap-2 mb-8 text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-slate-400 no-underline hover:text-slate-900 transition-colors bg-slate-50 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg w-max"
        >
          &larr; {dict.mapa.voltar}
        </Link>

        <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-8">
          <h2 className="text-2xl font-black text-slate-900 tracking-tight">
            {dict.mapa.filtros}
          </h2>
          <span className="text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-slate-500 bg-slate-100 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 shadow-sm">
            {camposExibidos.length} {dict.mapa.encontrados}
          </span>
        </div>

        <form action={`/${lang}/mapa`} method="GET" className="flex flex-col gap-5">
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-1.5">
            <label className="text-[10px] font-bold text-slate-500 uppercase tracking-widest ml-1">{dict.mapa.categoria}</label>
            <select name="categoria" defaultValue={categoria} className="w-full p-3.5 bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl text-sm font-bold text-slate-700 outline-none focus:bg-white focus:border-slate-900 cursor-pointer transition-colors">
              <option value="">{dict.mapa.todas}</option>
              {categoriasOpcoes.map((cat) => <option key={cat.valor} value={cat.valor}>{cat.label}</option>)}
            </select>
          </div>

          <div className="flex flex-col gap-1.5">
            <label className="text-[10px] font-bold text-slate-500 uppercase tracking-widest ml-1">{dict.mapa.idade}</label>
            <select name="idade" defaultValue={idade} className="w-full p-3.5 bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl text-sm font-bold text-slate-700 outline-none focus:bg-white focus:border-slate-900 cursor-pointer transition-colors">
              <option value="">{dict.mapa.qualquer_idade}</option>
              {idadesOpcoes.map((id) => <option key={id.valor} value={id.valor}>{id.label}</option>)}
            </select>
          </div>

          <div className="flex flex-col gap-1.5">
            <label className="text-[10px] font-bold text-slate-500 uppercase tracking-widest ml-1">{dict.mapa.linguas}</label>
            <select name="lingua" defaultValue={lingua} className="w-full p-3.5 bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl text-sm font-bold text-slate-700 outline-none focus:bg-white focus:border-slate-900 cursor-pointer transition-colors">
              <option value="">{dict.mapa.qualquer_lingua}</option>
              {linguasUnicas.map((lin) => <option key={lin} value={lin}>{lin}</option>)}
            </select>
          </div>

          <div className="flex flex-col gap-3 mt-4">
            <button type="submit" className="w-full bg-[#167524] hover:bg-[#115a1b] text-white font-black py-4 rounded-xl text-sm transition-all shadow-md">
              {dict.mapa.atualizar}
            </button>
            {temFiltrosAtivos && (
              <Link href={`/${lang}/mapa`} className="text-xs font-bold text-red-600 hover:text-red-700 uppercase tracking-widest text-center transition-colors">
                {dict.mapa.limpar}
              </Link>
            )}
          </div>
        </form>
      </aside>

      {/* ÁREA DO MAPA */}
      <section className="flex-1 relative min-h-[55vh] md:min-h-0 bg-slate-200">
        <MapaWrapper campos={camposExibidos || []} lang={lang} />
      </section>
    </main>
  );
}
</file>

<file path="app/[lang]/[categoria]/[localizacao]/page.tsx">
import { supabase } from "@/lib/supabase";
import Link from "next/link";
import BotaoFavorito from "../../(public)/components/BotaoFavorito";
import { unstable_cache } from "next/cache";

export const dynamic = 'force-dynamic';

const getCamposCacheados = unstable_cache(
  async () => {
    const { data } = await supabase
      .from("campos")
      .select("*")
      .not("contrato_parceiro_url", "is", null)
      .order("nome");
    return data || [];
  },
  ['cache-pesquisa-campos'],
  { revalidate: 86400 }
);

export async function generateMetadata({ params }: { params: Promise<{ lang: string, categoria: string, localizacao: string }> }) {
  const { lang, categoria, localizacao } = await params;
  
  const catFormatada = decodeURIComponent(categoria).replace(/-/g, ' ').replace(/\b\w/g, c => c.toUpperCase());
  const localFormatado = decodeURIComponent(localizacao).replace(/-/g, ' ').replace(/\b\w/g, c => c.toUpperCase());
  
  const isEn = lang === 'en';

  const todosOsCampos = await getCamposCacheados();
  const camposFiltrados = todosOsCampos.filter(c => {
    const matchCat = (c.categoria || '').toLowerCase().includes(catFormatada.toLowerCase()) || (c.categoria_en || '').toLowerCase().includes(catFormatada.toLowerCase());
    const matchLoc = (c.Distrito || c.local || c.pais || c.pais_en || '').toLowerCase().includes(localFormatado.toLowerCase());
    return matchCat && matchLoc;
  });

  const count = camposFiltrados.length;
  
  const metaTitle = isEn 
    ? `Best ${catFormatada} Camps in ${localFormatado} | HelloCamp` 
    : `Campos de Férias de ${catFormatada} em ${localFormatado} | HelloCamp`;
    
  const metaDescription = count > 0
    ? (isEn 
        ? `Compare the top ${count} ${catFormatada} camps in ${localFormatado}. View dates, prices, and reviews. Book safely with no commission on HelloCamp.`
        : `Compare os ${count} melhores campos de férias de ${catFormatada} em ${localFormatado}. Veja preços, fotos e datas. Reserve sem comissões na HelloCamp.`)
    : (isEn
        ? `Discover the best ${catFormatada} camps in ${localFormatado}. Find dates and prices for international summer experiences on HelloCamp.`
        : `Descubra os melhores campos de férias de ${catFormatada} em ${localFormatado}. Compare preços e reserve experiências de verão na HelloCamp.`);

  return {
    title: metaTitle,
    description: metaDescription,
    alternates: {
      canonical: `https://www.hellocamp.pt/${lang}/${categoria}/${localizacao}`
    }
  };
}

export default async function CategoriaLocalizacaoPage({ params }: { params: Promise<{ lang: string, categoria: string, localizacao: string }> }) {
  const { lang, categoria, localizacao } = await params;
  const isEn = lang === 'en';

  const catFormatada = decodeURIComponent(categoria).replace(/-/g, ' ').replace(/\b\w/g, c => c.toUpperCase());
  const localFormatado = decodeURIComponent(localizacao).replace(/-/g, ' ').replace(/\b\w/g, c => c.toUpperCase());

  const todosOsCampos = await getCamposCacheados();
  
  const resultados = todosOsCampos.filter(c => {
    const matchCat = (c.categoria || '').toLowerCase().includes(catFormatada.toLowerCase()) || (c.categoria_en || '').toLowerCase().includes(catFormatada.toLowerCase());
    const matchLoc = (c.Distrito || c.local || c.pais || c.pais_en || c.local_en || '').toLowerCase().includes(localFormatado.toLowerCase());
    return matchCat && matchLoc;
  });

  return (
    <main className="min-h-screen bg-slate-50 text-slate-900 pb-20 font-sans">
      
      <section className="bg-white border-b border-slate-200 pt-8 pb-10 px-4 md:px-6 shadow-sm">
        <div className="max-w-[1100px] mx-auto">
          <nav className="flex items-center gap-2 text-[10px] font-black text-slate-400 mb-6 tracking-widest uppercase">
            <Link href={`/${lang}`} className="hover:text-[#0E3A68] transition-colors">{isEn ? 'Home' : 'Início'}</Link>
            <span>/</span>
            <Link href={`/${lang}/pesquisa`} className="hover:text-[#0E3A68] transition-colors">{isEn ? 'Search' : 'Pesquisa'}</Link>
            <span>/</span>
            <span className="text-slate-900">{catFormatada}</span>
            <span>/</span>
            <span className="text-slate-900">{localFormatado}</span>
          </nav>

          <h1 className="text-3xl md:text-5xl font-black text-slate-900 tracking-tight leading-tight">
            {isEn ? `${catFormatada} Camps in ${localFormatado}` : `Campos de Férias de ${catFormatada} em ${localFormatado}`}
          </h1>
          <p className="mt-3 text-slate-500 font-medium">
            {resultados.length > 0 
              ? (isEn ? `Showing ${resultados.length} available programs.` : `A mostrar ${resultados.length} programas disponíveis.`)
              : (isEn ? `No specific programs found at the moment.` : `Sem programas abertos no momento.`)}
          </p>
        </div>
      </section>

      <section className="max-w-[1100px] mx-auto px-4 py-12 md:py-16">
        {resultados.length === 0 ? (
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-16">
            <div className="text-center p-12 bg-white rounded-[2rem] border border-slate-200 shadow-sm flex flex-col items-center gap-4 max-w-2xl mx-auto">
              <div className="w-20 h-20 bg-amber-50 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center text-4xl border border-amber-100 mb-2">
                🗺️
              </div>
              <h3 className="text-2xl font-black text-slate-900 tracking-tight">
                {isEn ? `No exact matches for ${catFormatada} in ${localFormatado}` : `Ainda não temos campos exatos de ${catFormatada} em ${localFormatado}`}
              </h3>
              <p className="text-base text-slate-500 font-medium">
                {isEn 
                  ? 'We are constantly adding new partners. Meanwhile, check out these amazing alternatives that other families are booking right now.' 
                  : 'Estamos a adicionar parceiros todos os dias. Entretanto, veja estas alternativas incríveis que outras famílias estão a reservar.'}
              </p>
              <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row gap-4 mt-6 w-full sm:w-auto">
                <Link href={`/${lang}/pesquisa?categoria=${encodeURIComponent(catFormatada)}`} className="bg-[#e2a41d] text-white font-black px-8 py-4 rounded-xl hover:bg-[#d4991b] transition-all shadow-lg uppercase tracking-widest text-xs flex-1 text-center">
                  {isEn ? `Search Anywhere` : `Procurar Noutros Locais`}
                </Link>
                <Link href={`/${lang}/pesquisa`} className="bg-[#0E3A68] text-white font-black px-8 py-4 rounded-xl hover:bg-[#0b2b4d] transition-all shadow-lg uppercase tracking-widest text-xs flex-1 text-center border border-[#0E3A68]">
                  {isEn ? 'View All Camps' : 'Ver Todos os Campos'}
                </Link>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Alternativas Recomendadas */}
            <div>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-4 mb-8">
                <h4 className="text-2xl font-black text-slate-900 tracking-tight">
                  {isEn ? 'Recommended Alternatives' : 'Alternativas Recomendadas'}
                </h4>
                <div className="flex-1 h-px bg-slate-200"></div>
              </div>
              
              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-3 gap-8">
                {todosOsCampos.slice(0, 3).map((campo) => {
                  const nomeCampo = isEn && campo.nome_en ? campo.nome_en : campo.nome;
                  const catCampo = isEn && campo.categoria_en ? campo.categoria_en : campo.categoria;
                  const localC = isEn && campo.local_en ? campo.local_en : (campo.Distrito || campo.local);
                  let precoVisivel = campo.preco || 0;
                  if (!precoVisivel && campo.pacotes?.length > 0) {
                    const todosPrecos = campo.pacotes.flatMap((p: any) => p.variantes?.map((v: any) => v.preco) || []);
                    if (todosPrecos.length > 0) precoVisivel = Math.min(...todosPrecos);
                  }

                  const imgUrl = campo.imagem?.startsWith('http') ? campo.imagem : '/og-image.jpg';

                  return (
                    <div key={campo.id} className="group flex flex-col bg-white rounded-[2rem] overflow-hidden border border-slate-200 relative shadow-sm hover:shadow-[0_20px_60px_rgb(0,0,0,0.08)] transition-all duration-300 hover:-translate-y-1">
                      <Link href={`/${lang}/campo/${campo.id}`} className="absolute inset-0 z-10"><span className="sr-only">Explorar {nomeCampo}</span></Link>
                      <div className="absolute top-4 right-4 z-20"><BotaoFavorito campoId={campo.id} /></div>
                      <div className="relative h-60 w-full overflow-hidden bg-slate-100">
                        <div 
                          className="absolute inset-0 w-full h-full bg-cover bg-center transition-transform duration-700 group-hover:scale-105"
                          style={{ backgroundImage: `url('${imgUrl}')` }}
                        />
                        <div className="absolute top-4 left-4 bg-white/90 backdrop-blur-sm px-3 py-1.5 text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-[#0E3A68] rounded-lg z-0">{catCampo}</div>
                      </div>
                      <div className="flex flex-col p-6 flex-1 pointer-events-none">
                        <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[10px] font-black text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-widest mb-3">
                          <svg className="w-3 h-3 text-[#e2a41d]" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="3"><path d="M21 10c0 7-9 13-9 13s-9-6-9-13a9 9 0 0 1 18 0z"></path><circle cx="12" cy="10" r="3"></circle></svg>
                          {localC}
                        </div>
                        <h3 className="text-xl font-black text-slate-900 leading-tight mb-2 line-clamp-2">{nomeCampo}</h3>
                        <div className="mt-auto pt-6 flex items-center justify-between border-t border-slate-100">
                          <div>
                            <p className="text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-slate-400 mb-0.5">{isEn ? 'From' : 'Desde'}</p>
                            <p className="text-2xl font-black text-[#0E3A68] m-0">{precoVisivel > 0 ? `${precoVisivel}€` : '--'}</p>
                          </div>
                          <span className="bg-slate-100 text-slate-900 px-5 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest transition-colors group-hover:bg-[#0E3A68] group-hover:text-white shadow-sm">
                            {isEn ? 'View Dates' : 'Ver Datas'}
                          </span>
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  );
                })}
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-3 gap-8">
            {resultados.map((campo) => {
              const nomeCampo = isEn && campo.nome_en ? campo.nome_en : campo.nome;
              const catCampo = isEn && campo.categoria_en ? campo.categoria_en : campo.categoria;
              const localCampo = isEn && campo.local_en ? campo.local_en : (campo.Distrito || campo.local);
              const idadeCampo = isEn && campo.idade_en ? campo.idade_en : campo.idade;

              let precoVisivel = campo.preco || 0;
              if (!precoVisivel && campo.pacotes?.length > 0) {
                const todosPrecos = campo.pacotes.flatMap((p: any) => p.variantes?.map((v: any) => v.preco) || []);
                if (todosPrecos.length > 0) precoVisivel = Math.min(...todosPrecos);
              }

              const imgUrl = campo.imagem?.startsWith('http') ? campo.imagem : '/og-image.jpg';

              return (
                <div key={campo.id} className="group flex flex-col bg-white rounded-[2rem] overflow-hidden border border-slate-200 relative shadow-sm hover:shadow-[0_20px_60px_rgb(0,0,0,0.08)] transition-all duration-300 hover:-translate-y-1">
                  <Link href={`/${lang}/campo/${campo.id}`} className="absolute inset-0 z-10"><span className="sr-only">Explorar {nomeCampo}</span></Link>
                  <div className="absolute top-4 right-4 z-20"><BotaoFavorito campoId={campo.id} /></div>
                  
                  <div className="relative h-60 w-full overflow-hidden bg-slate-100">
                    <div 
                      className="absolute inset-0 w-full h-full bg-cover bg-center transition-transform duration-700 group-hover:scale-105"
                      style={{ backgroundImage: `url('${imgUrl}')` }}
                    />
                    <div className="absolute top-4 left-4 bg-white/90 backdrop-blur-sm px-3 py-1.5 text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-[#0E3A68] rounded-lg z-0 shadow-sm">
                      {catCampo}
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <div className="flex flex-col p-6 flex-1 pointer-events-none">
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[10px] font-black text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-widest mb-3">
                      <svg className="w-3 h-3 text-[#e2a41d]" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="3"><path d="M21 10c0 7-9 13-9 13s-9-6-9-13a9 9 0 0 1 18 0z"></path><circle cx="12" cy="10" r="3"></circle></svg>
                      {localCampo}
                    </div>
                    <h3 className="text-xl font-black text-slate-900 leading-tight mb-2 line-clamp-2">{nomeCampo}</h3>
                    <p className="text-sm text-slate-500 font-medium mb-6">{isEn ? 'Ages:' : 'Idades:'} <span className="text-slate-900 font-bold">{idadeCampo}</span></p>
                    
                    <div className="mt-auto pt-6 flex items-center justify-between border-t border-slate-100">
                      <div>
                        <p className="text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-slate-400 mb-0.5">{isEn ? 'From' : 'Desde'}</p>
                        <p className="text-2xl font-black text-[#0E3A68] m-0">{precoVisivel > 0 ? `${precoVisivel}€` : '--'}</p>
                      </div>
                      <span className="bg-slate-100 text-slate-900 px-5 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest transition-colors group-hover:bg-[#0E3A68] group-hover:text-white shadow-sm">
                        {isEn ? 'View Dates' : 'Ver Datas'}
                      </span>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>
              );
            })}
          </div>
        )}
      </section>
    </main>
  );
}
</file>

<file path="app/[lang]/(public)/blog/[slug]/page.tsx">
import { Metadata } from "next";
import { supabase } from "@/lib/supabase";
import Link from "next/link";
import { notFound } from "next/navigation";
// Importação normal (O Next.js já sabe que é um Client Component devido ao "use client" dentro dele)
import BotaoPartilhaArtigo from "../components/BotaoPartilhaArtigo";

const FALLBACK_COVER = "https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1544367567-0f2fcb009e0b?auto=format&fit=crop&q=80&w=1200";

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════
// METADADOS SEO (BLINDADO)
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════
export async function generateMetadata({ params }: { params: Promise<{ lang: string; slug: string }> }): Promise<Metadata> {
  const { lang, slug } = await params;
  const isEn = lang === 'en';

  try {
    const { data: post } = await supabase
      .from('blog_posts')
      .select('titulo, titulo_en, resumo, resumo_en, imagem_url, created_at')
      .eq('slug', decodeURIComponent(slug))
      .limit(1)
      .maybeSingle();

    if (!post) return { title: 'Blog | HelloCamp' };

    const title = (isEn && post.titulo_en ? post.titulo_en : (post.titulo || 'Blog')) + ' | HelloCamp';
    const description = isEn && post.resumo_en ? post.resumo_en : (post.resumo || '');

    return {
      title,
      description,
      openGraph: {
        title,
        description,
        type: 'article',
        publishedTime: post.created_at || new Date().toISOString(),
        images: [{ url: post.imagem_url || FALLBACK_COVER, width: 1200, height: 630 }]
      }
    };
  } catch (error) {
    return { title: 'Blog | HelloCamp' };
  }
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════
// PÁGINA PRINCIPAL DO ARTIGO
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════
export default async function BlogPostPage({ params }: { params: Promise<{ lang: string; slug: string }> }) {
  const { lang, slug } = await params;
  const isEn = lang === 'en';

  let post = null;

  try {
    const { data, error } = await supabase
      .from('blog_posts')
      .select('*')
      .eq('slug', decodeURIComponent(slug))
      .limit(1)
      .maybeSingle();

    if (error) throw error;
    post = data;
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Erro interno ao carregar o artigo:", error);
  }

  // Se o artigo não for encontrado, aciona a página 404 elegantemente
  if (!post) {
    notFound();
  }

  // Fallbacks para garantir que a página nunca crasha
  const titulo = isEn && post.titulo_en ? post.titulo_en : (post.titulo || 'Artigo sem título');
  const conteudo = isEn && post.conteudo_en ? post.conteudo_en : (post.conteudo || '<p>Conteúdo em atualização...</p>');
  const categoria = isEn && post.categoria_en ? post.categoria_en : (post.categoria || '');

  // Formatação segura da data
  let dataPublicacao = '';
  try {
    if (post.created_at) {
      const d = new Date(post.created_at);
      dataPublicacao = `${d.getDate().toString().padStart(2, '0')}/${(d.getMonth() + 1).toString().padStart(2, '0')}/${d.getFullYear()}`;
    }
  } catch (e) {
    dataPublicacao = 'Recente';
  }

  // Schema de SEO injetado de forma segura
  const articleSchema = {
    "@context": "https://schema.org",
    "@type": "BlogPosting",
    "headline": titulo,
    "image": post.imagem_url || FALLBACK_COVER,
    "datePublished": post.created_at || new Date().toISOString(),
    "author": { "@type": "Organization", "name": "HelloCamp" }
  };
  const schemaString = JSON.stringify(articleSchema).replace(/</g, '\\u003c');

  return (
    <main className="min-h-screen bg-white font-sans text-slate-900 pb-24">
      <script type="application/ld+json" dangerouslySetInnerHTML={{ __html: schemaString }} />

      {/* CABEÇALHO DO ARTIGO */}
      <header className="max-w-4xl mx-auto px-4 md:px-6 pt-16 pb-12 text-center">
        <Link
          href={`/${lang}/blog`}
          className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-900 bg-slate-50 hover:bg-slate-100 px-5 py-2.5 rounded-full mb-10 transition-colors border border-slate-200 shadow-sm"
        >
          &larr; {isEn ? 'Back to Journal' : 'Voltar aos Guias'}
        </Link>

        {categoria && (
          <div className="mb-4 text-[11px] font-black text-[#0E3A68] uppercase tracking-widest bg-blue-50 border border-blue-100 px-3 py-1 rounded-md inline-block">
            {categoria}
          </div>
        )}

        <h1 className="text-4xl md:text-5xl lg:text-6xl font-black tracking-tight leading-[1.1] mb-8 text-slate-900">
          {titulo}
        </h1>

        <div className="flex items-center justify-center gap-4 text-xs font-bold text-slate-500 uppercase tracking-widest flex-wrap">
          {dataPublicacao && <span>{dataPublicacao}</span>}
          <span className="text-slate-300">•</span>
          <span className="text-slate-400">{isEn ? 'HelloCamp Editorial' : 'Redação HelloCamp'}</span>
        </div>
      </header>

      {/* IMAGEM DE CAPA (Segura sem funções de cliente) */}
      <div className="max-w-5xl mx-auto px-4 md:px-6 mb-16">
        <div className="w-full aspect-[16/9] md:aspect-[21/9] rounded-[2.5rem] overflow-hidden shadow-2xl border border-slate-100 bg-slate-100">
          <img
            src={post.imagem_url || FALLBACK_COVER}
            alt={titulo}
            className="w-full h-full object-cover"
          />
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* CORPO DO ARTIGO */}
      <article className="
        max-w-3xl mx-auto px-4 md:px-6
        prose prose-slate prose-lg md:prose-xl w-full
        prose-headings:font-black prose-headings:tracking-tight prose-headings:text-slate-900
        prose-h2:text-3xl prose-h2:mt-16 prose-h2:mb-6
        prose-h3:text-2xl prose-h3:mt-12 prose-h3:mb-4
        prose-p:text-slate-600 prose-p:leading-relaxed prose-p:mb-8
        prose-a:text-[#0E3A68] prose-a:font-bold prose-a:no-underline hover:prose-a:underline
        prose-strong:font-black prose-strong:text-slate-900
        prose-li:text-slate-600 prose-li:marker:text-[#0E3A68]
        prose-img:rounded-3xl prose-img:shadow-xl prose-img:border prose-img:border-slate-100
      ">
        <div dangerouslySetInnerHTML={{ __html: conteudo || '' }} />
      </article>

      {/* RODAPÉ DO ARTIGO / PARTILHA */}
      <div className="max-w-3xl mx-auto px-4 md:px-6 mt-16 pb-10">
        <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-center justify-between gap-6 bg-slate-50 border border-slate-100 rounded-3xl p-8">
          <div>
            <p className="text-base font-black text-slate-900 mb-1">
              {isEn ? 'Did you find this helpful?' : 'Achou este artigo útil?'}
            </p>
            <p className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-500 m-0">
              {isEn ? 'Share it with your network.' : 'Partilhe o guia para ajudar outras famílias ou parceiros.'}
            </p>
          </div>
          <div className="bg-white p-2 rounded-xl shadow-sm border border-slate-200 flex items-center justify-center">
             <BotaoPartilhaArtigo title={titulo} isEn={isEn} layout="button" />
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* BOTTOM CROSS-SELLING */}
      <div className="max-w-4xl mx-auto px-4 md:px-6 mt-10 pt-16 border-t border-slate-200">
        <div className="bg-[#0E3A68] rounded-[2.5rem] p-10 md:p-14 text-center shadow-2xl relative overflow-hidden">
          <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-[radial-gradient(ellipse_at_top,_var(--tw-gradient-stops))] from-blue-800 to-[#0E3A68] pointer-events-none" />
          
          <div className="relative z-10">
            <span className="text-4xl mb-6 block">🏕️</span>
            <h2 className="text-3xl md:text-4xl font-black text-white tracking-tight mb-4">
              {isEn ? 'Ready for the next adventure?' : 'Pronto para a próxima aventura?'}
            </h2>
            <p className="text-blue-100 text-lg font-medium mb-10 max-w-xl mx-auto">
              {isEn 
                ? `Don't wait until it's fully booked. Discover the best ${categoria || ''} summer camps on HelloCamp.` 
                : `Não espere que esgote. Compare, escolha e reserve de forma segura na maior plataforma ibérica.`}
            </p>
            <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row gap-4 justify-center">
              <Link 
                href={`/${lang}/pesquisa${categoria ? `?categoria=${encodeURIComponent(categoria)}` : ''}`} 
                className="bg-[#e2a41d] hover:bg-[#d4991b] text-slate-900 font-black px-10 py-5 rounded-2xl transition-all shadow-xl shadow-[#e2a41d]/20 uppercase tracking-widest text-sm"
              >
                {isEn ? 'Search Camps Now' : 'Pesquisar Campos Agora'}
              </Link>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

    </main>
  );
}
