<file path="app/[lang]/(public)/pesquisa/page.tsx">
import { supabase } from "@/lib/supabase";
import Link from "next/link";
import BotaoFavorito from "../components/BotaoFavorito";
import { unstable_cache } from "next/cache";

export const dynamic = 'force-dynamic';

const escapeOr = (str: any) => (typeof str === 'string' ? str.replace(/[,()]/g, ' ').trim() : '');

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════
// CACHE DE DADOS (24 HORAS) - ZERO EGRESS
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════
const getCamposCacheados = unstable_cache(
  async () => {
    const { data } = await supabase
      .from("campos")
      .select("*")
      .not("contrato_parceiro_url", "is", null)
      .order("nome");
    return data || [];
  },
  ['cache-pesquisa-campos'],
  { revalidate: 86400 } // 24 horas
);

// ==========================================
// COMPONENTE SERVIDOR DE ALTA PERFORMANCE
// ==========================================
export default async function PaginaPesquisa({
  params,
  searchParams,
}: {
  params: Promise<{ lang: string }>;
  searchParams: Promise<{ [key: string]: string | string[] | undefined }>;
}) {
  const { lang } = await params;
  const isEn = lang === 'en';
  const resolvedSearchParams = await searchParams;

  const qParam = (resolvedSearchParams.q as string) || "";
  const catParam = (resolvedSearchParams.categoria as string) || "";
  const distParam = (resolvedSearchParams.distrito as string) || "";
  const idadeParam = (resolvedSearchParams.idade as string) || "";
  const paisParam = (resolvedSearchParams.pais as string) || "";
  const dataParam = (resolvedSearchParams.data as string) || "";

  const mostrarDistritos = paisParam === "" || paisParam.toLowerCase() === "portugal";
  const filtrosAtivos = !!(qParam || catParam || distParam || idadeParam || paisParam || dataParam);

  const todosOsCampos = await getCamposCacheados();

  const catSet = new Set<string>();
  const idadeSet = new Set<string>();
  
  todosOsCampos.forEach(c => {
    if (c.categoria?.trim()) catSet.add(c.categoria.trim());
    if (c.idade?.trim()) idadeSet.add(c.idade.trim());
  });
  
  const categoriasDisponiveis = Array.from(catSet).sort();
  const idadesDisponiveis = Array.from(idadeSet).sort();

  let resultados = todosOsCampos;

  if (qParam) {
    const termos = escapeOr(qParam).toLowerCase().split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean);
    resultados = resultados.filter(campo => {
      const textoBuscavel = `${campo.nome || ''} ${campo.descricao || ''} ${campo.categoria || ''} ${campo.local || ''} ${campo.Distrito || ''} ${campo.pais || ''} ${campo.pais_en || ''} ${campo.nome_en || ''} ${campo.descricao_en || ''}`.toLowerCase();
      return termos.every(termo => textoBuscavel.includes(termo));
    });
  }

  if (catParam) {
    resultados = resultados.filter(c => c.categoria === catParam);
  }
  
  if (distParam && mostrarDistritos) {
    resultados = resultados.filter(c => (c.Distrito || '').toLowerCase().includes(escapeOr(distParam).toLowerCase()));
  }
  
  if (idadeParam) {
    resultados = resultados.filter(c => c.idade === idadeParam);
  }
  
  // FILTRAGEM RIGOROSA PELA COLUNA DE PAÍS (PT e EN)
  if (paisParam) {
    resultados = resultados.filter(c => {
      const p1 = (c.pais || '').toLowerCase();
      const p2 = (c.pais_en || '').toLowerCase();
      const termo = escapeOr(paisParam).toLowerCase();
      return p1.includes(termo) || p2.includes(termo);
    });
  }
  
  if (dataParam) {
    resultados = resultados.filter(c => {
      if (!c.data_inicio) return false;
      return new Date(c.data_inicio) >= new Date(dataParam);
    });
  }

  const distritosPT = ["Aveiro", "Beja", "Braga", "Bragança", "Castelo Branco", "Coimbra", "Évora", "Faro", "Guarda", "Leiria", "Lisboa", "Portalegre", "Porto", "Santarém", "Setúbal", "Viana do Castelo", "Vila Real", "Viseu"];
  const todayISO = new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0];

  const baseInputClass = "w-full p-3.5 bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl text-sm font-bold text-slate-700 outline-none focus:border-[#0E3A68] focus:bg-white transition-colors shadow-sm cursor-pointer hover:border-slate-300";
  const customSelectClass = `${baseInputClass} pr-10 appearance-none bg-[url('data:image/svg+xml;charset=US-ASCII,%3Csvg%20xmlns%3D%22http%3A%2F%2Fwww.w3.org%2F2000%2Fsvg%22%20width%3D%22292.4%22%20height%3D%22292.4%22%3E%3Cpath%20fill%3D%22%2364748b%22%20d%3D%22M287%2069.4a17.6%2017.6%200%200%200-13-5.4H18.4c-5%200-9.3%201.8-12.9%205.4A17.6%2017.6%200%200%200%200%2082.2c0%205%201.8%209.3%205.4%2012.9l128%20127.9c3.6%203.6%207.8%205.4%2012.8%205.4s9.2-1.8%2012.8-5.4L287%2095c3.5-3.5%205.4-7.8%205.4-12.8%200-5-1.9-9.2-5.5-12.8z%22%2F%3E%3C%2Fsvg%3E')] bg-[length:0.75rem_auto] bg-[position:right_1rem_center] bg-no-repeat`;

  return (
    <main className="min-h-screen bg-slate-50 text-slate-900 pb-20 font-sans">
      
      {/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════ */}
      {/* BARRA DE PESQUISA SUPERIOR (FILTROS)        */}
      {/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════ */}
      <section className="bg-white border-b border-slate-200 pt-8 pb-10 px-4 md:px-6 shadow-sm relative z-20">
        <div className="max-w-[1100px] mx-auto">
          <nav className="flex items-center gap-2 text-[10px] font-black text-slate-400 mb-6 tracking-widest uppercase">
            <Link href={`/${lang}`} className="hover:text-[#0E3A68] transition-colors">{isEn ? 'Home' : 'Início'}</Link>
            <span>/</span>
            <span className="text-slate-900">{isEn ? 'Search' : 'Pesquisa'}</span>
          </nav>

          <h1 className="text-3xl md:text-4xl font-black text-slate-900 mb-8 tracking-tight">
            {filtrosAtivos ? (isEn ? 'Search Results' : 'Resultados da Pesquisa') : (isEn ? 'Explore All Camps' : 'Explorar Todos os Campos')}
          </h1>

          <form method="GET" action={`/${lang}/pesquisa`} className="flex flex-col gap-6">
            
            <div className="relative w-full">
              <div className="absolute inset-y-0 left-0 pl-5 flex items-center pointer-events-none">
                <svg className="w-5 h-5 text-slate-400" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="3"><circle cx="11" cy="11" r="8"></circle><line x1="21" y1="21" x2="16.65" y2="16.65"></line></svg>
              </div>
              <input
                type="text"
                name="q"
                defaultValue={qParam}
                placeholder={isEn ? "Search by name, sport, or location (e.g., Surf in Porto)..." : "Pesquise por nome, desporto ou local (ex: Surf no Porto)..."}
                className="w-full pl-14 pr-6 py-4 bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-2xl text-base font-bold text-slate-900 outline-none focus:border-[#0E3A68] focus:bg-white focus:ring-4 focus:ring-[#0E3A68]/10 transition-all shadow-sm placeholder:text-slate-400 placeholder:font-medium hover:border-slate-300"
              />
            </div>

            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-3 lg:grid-cols-6 gap-4 items-end">
              <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
                <label className="text-[10px] font-black text-slate-500 uppercase tracking-widest ml-1">{isEn ? 'Country' : 'País'}</label>
                <select name="pais" defaultValue={paisParam} className={customSelectClass}>
                  <option value="">{isEn ? 'All Countries' : 'Todos'}</option>
                  <option value="Portugal">Portugal</option>
                  <option value="Espanha">{isEn ? 'Spain' : 'Espanha'}</option>
                  <option value="Reino Unido">{isEn ? 'United Kingdom' : 'Reino Unido'}</option>
                  <option value="França">{isEn ? 'France' : 'França'}</option>
                </select>
              </div>

              <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
                <label className="text-[10px] font-black text-slate-500 uppercase tracking-widest ml-1">{isEn ? 'Category' : 'Categoria'}</label>
                <select name="categoria" defaultValue={catParam} className={customSelectClass}>
                  <option value="">{isEn ? 'All Categories' : 'Todas'}</option>
                  {categoriasDisponiveis.map(c => <option key={c} value={c}>{c}</option>)}
                </select>
              </div>

              {mostrarDistritos && (
                <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
                  <label className="text-[10px] font-black text-slate-500 uppercase tracking-widest ml-1">Distrito</label>
                  <select name="distrito" defaultValue={distParam} className={customSelectClass}>
                    <option value="">{isEn ? 'All Districts' : 'Todos'}</option>
                    {distritosPT.map(d => <option key={d} value={d}>{d}</option>)}
                  </select>
                </div>
              )}

              <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
                <label className="text-[10px] font-black text-slate-500 uppercase tracking-widest ml-1">{isEn ? 'Age Group' : 'Idade'}</label>
                <select name="idade" defaultValue={idadeParam} className={customSelectClass}>
                  <option value="">{isEn ? 'All Ages' : 'Todas'}</option>
                  {idadesDisponiveis.map(i => <option key={i} value={i}>{i}</option>)}
                </select>
              </div>

              <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
                <label className="text-[10px] font-black text-slate-500 uppercase tracking-widest ml-1">{isEn ? 'Start Date' : 'Data Início'}</label>
                <input
                  type="date"
                  name="data"
                  defaultValue={dataParam}
                  min={todayISO}
                  className={baseInputClass}
                />
              </div>

              <button type="submit" className="w-full py-3.5 bg-[#e2a41d] hover:bg-[#d4991b] text-white font-black text-xs uppercase tracking-widest rounded-xl transition-all shadow-md mt-auto hover:-translate-y-0.5">
                {isEn ? 'Search' : 'Pesquisar'}
              </button>
            </div>

            {filtrosAtivos && (
              <div className="text-right mt-1">
                <Link href={`/${lang}/pesquisa`} className="text-[10px] font-black text-red-600 hover:text-red-700 transition-colors uppercase tracking-widest bg-red-50 hover:bg-red-100 px-4 py-2 rounded-lg border border-red-100 inline-block shadow-sm">
                  ✕ {isEn ? 'Clear filters' : 'Limpar filtros'}
                </Link>
              </div>
            )}
          </form>
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════ */}
      {/* GRID DE RESULTADOS OTIMIZADA PARA CONVERSÃO */}
      {/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════ */}
      <section className="max-w-[1100px] mx-auto px-4 py-12 md:py-16">
        {(!resultados || resultados.length === 0) ? (
          <div className="text-center p-12 bg-white rounded-[2rem] border border-slate-200 shadow-sm flex flex-col items-center gap-4 max-w-2xl mx-auto">
            <div className="w-20 h-20 bg-slate-50 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center text-4xl border border-slate-100 mb-2">🏕️</div>
            <h3 className="text-2xl font-black text-slate-900 tracking-tight">{isEn ? 'No camps found' : 'Nenhum programa encontrado'}</h3>
            <p className="text-sm text-slate-500 font-medium">
              {isEn ? 'Try adjusting your filters, selecting a different date, or searching for something more generic.' : 'Não encontrámos resultados exatos. Tente remover o filtro da data ou pesquisar de forma mais genérica (Ex: Futebol).'}
            </p>
            <Link href={`/${lang}/pesquisa`} className="mt-4 bg-[#0E3A68] text-white font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-[10px] px-8 py-4 rounded-xl hover:bg-[#0b2b4d] transition-colors shadow-lg">
              {isEn ? 'Clear all filters' : 'Ver todos os programas'}
            </Link>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-3 gap-8">
            {resultados.map((campo) => {
              const nomeCampo = isEn && campo.nome_en ? campo.nome_en : campo.nome;
              const catCampo = isEn && campo.categoria_en ? campo.categoria_en : campo.categoria;
              const localCampo = isEn && campo.local_en ? campo.local_en : (campo.Distrito || campo.local);
              const idadeCampo = isEn && campo.idade_en ? campo.idade_en : campo.idade;

              let precoVisivel = campo.preco || 0;
              if (!precoVisivel && campo.pacotes?.length > 0) {
                const todosPrecos = campo.pacotes.flatMap((p: any) => p.variantes?.map((v: any) => v.preco) || []);
                if (todosPrecos.length > 0) precoVisivel = Math.min(...todosPrecos);
              }

              const imgUrl = campo.imagem?.startsWith('http') ? campo.imagem : '/og-image.jpg';

              return (
                <div key={campo.id} className="group flex flex-col bg-white rounded-[2rem] overflow-hidden border border-slate-200 relative shadow-sm hover:shadow-[0_20px_60px_rgb(0,0,0,0.08)] transition-all duration-300 hover:-translate-y-1">
                  <Link href={`/${lang}/campo/${campo.id}`} className="absolute inset-0 z-10"><span className="sr-only">Explorar {nomeCampo}</span></Link>
                  <div className="absolute top-4 right-4 z-20"><BotaoFavorito campoId={campo.id} /></div>
                  
                  <div className="relative h-60 w-full overflow-hidden bg-slate-100">
                    <div 
                      className="absolute inset-0 w-full h-full bg-cover bg-center transition-transform duration-700 group-hover:scale-105"
                      style={{ backgroundImage: `url('${imgUrl}')` }}
                    />
                    <div className="absolute top-4 left-4 bg-white/90 backdrop-blur-sm px-3 py-1.5 text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-[#0E3A68] rounded-lg z-0 shadow-sm">
                      {catCampo}
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <div className="flex flex-col p-6 flex-1 pointer-events-none">
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[10px] font-black text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-widest mb-3">
                      <svg className="w-3 h-3 text-[#e2a41d]" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="3"><path d="M21 10c0 7-9 13-9 13s-9-6-9-13a9 9 0 0 1 18 0z"></path><circle cx="12" cy="10" r="3"></circle></svg>
                      {localCampo}
                    </div>
                    <h3 className="text-xl font-black text-slate-900 leading-tight mb-2 line-clamp-2">{nomeCampo}</h3>
                    <p className="text-sm text-slate-500 font-medium mb-6">{isEn ? 'Ages:' : 'Idades:'} <span className="text-slate-900 font-bold">{idadeCampo}</span></p>
                    
                    <div className="mt-auto pt-6 flex items-center justify-between border-t border-slate-100">
                      <div>
                        <p className="text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-slate-400 mb-0.5">{isEn ? 'From' : 'A partir de'}</p>
                        <p className="text-2xl font-black text-[#0E3A68] m-0">{precoVisivel > 0 ? `${precoVisivel}€` : '--'}</p>
                      </div>
                      <span className="bg-slate-100 text-[#0E3A68] px-5 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest transition-colors group-hover:bg-[#0E3A68] group-hover:text-white shadow-sm">
                        {isEn ? 'View Dates' : 'Ver Datas'}
                      </span>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>
              );
            })}
          </div>
        )}
      </section>
    </main>
  );
}
</file>

<file path="app/[lang]/(public)/mapa/MapaMapLibre.tsx">
"use client";

import { useEffect, useRef, useMemo } from "react";
import * as maplibregl from "maplibre-gl";
import "maplibre-gl/dist/maplibre-gl.css";

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// WORKER DO MAPLIBRE
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
if (typeof window !== "undefined") {
  try {
    // @ts-ignore
    if (typeof (maplibregl as any).setWorkerUrl === "function") {
      (maplibregl as any).setWorkerUrl(
        "https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/maplibre-gl@5.0.1/dist/maplibre-gl-csp-worker.js"
      );
    }
  } catch (e) {
    console.warn("[MAPA] setWorkerUrl falhou:", e);
  }
}

interface Campo {
  id: string | number;
  nome: string;
  imagem?: string;
  categoria?: string;
  preco?: number;
  latitude: number;
  longitude: number;
}

interface MapaMapLibreProps {
  campos: any[];
  lang: string;
}

const FALLBACK_IMG =
  "data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg' viewBox='0 0 400 200'%3E%3Crect fill='%23f1f5f9' width='400' height='200'/%3E%3Ctext x='50%25' y='50%25' font-family='sans-serif' font-size='16' font-weight='bold' fill='%2394a3b8' text-anchor='middle' dy='.3em'%3EHelloCamp%3C/text%3E%3C/svg%3E";

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// ESTILO ESRI WORLD STREET MAP (Inglês, Grátis, Sem API Key)
// Design Premium e Zoom profundo até ao nível da rua.
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
const esriRasterStyle: maplibregl.StyleSpecification = {
  version: 8,
  sources: {
    'esri-tiles': {
      type: 'raster',
      tiles: [
        'https://server.arcgisonline.com/ArcGIS/rest/services/World_Street_Map/MapServer/tile/{z}/{y}/{x}'
      ],
      tileSize: 256,
      attribution: '&copy; <a href="https://www.esri.com/" target="_blank">Esri</a>, NAVTEQ, Garmin'
    }
  },
  layers: [
    {
      id: 'esri-tiles-layer',
      type: 'raster',
      source: 'esri-tiles',
      minzoom: 0,
      maxzoom: 19
    }
  ]
};

export default function MapaMapLibre({ campos, lang }: MapaMapLibreProps) {
  const mapContainer = useRef<HTMLDivElement | null>(null);
  const map = useRef<maplibregl.Map | null>(null);
  const isEn = lang === "en";

  const camposValidos = useMemo<Campo[]>(() => {
    const lista = campos || [];
    const contagemCoords: Record<string, number> = {};

    return lista
      .map((c): Campo | null => {
        const latBase = Number(c.latitude);
        const lngBase = Number(c.longitude);
        if (
          c.latitude == null || c.longitude == null ||
          c.latitude === "" || c.longitude === "" ||
          isNaN(latBase) || isNaN(lngBase) ||
          latBase === 0 || lngBase === 0
        ) return null;

        // Se dois campos tiverem exatamente a mesma coordenada, afasta-os ligeiramente em círculo (~35m)
        const chave = `${latBase.toFixed(4)},${lngBase.toFixed(4)}`;
        const ocorrencia = contagemCoords[chave] || 0;
        contagemCoords[chave] = ocorrencia + 1;

        let lat = latBase;
        let lng = lngBase;
        if (ocorrencia > 0) {
          const angulo = (ocorrencia * 2 * Math.PI) / 6;
          const raio = 0.00035 * Math.ceil(ocorrencia / 6);
          lat = latBase + Math.sin(angulo) * raio;
          lng = lngBase + Math.cos(angulo) * raio;
        }

        return {
          id: c.id,
          nome: isEn && c.nome_en ? c.nome_en : c.nome,
          imagem: c.imagem,
          categoria: isEn && c.categoria_en ? c.categoria_en : c.categoria,
          preco: Number(c.preco) || 0,
          latitude: lat,
          longitude: lng,
        };
      })
      .filter((c): c is Campo => c !== null);
  }, [campos, isEn]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!mapContainer.current || map.current) return;

    const instance = new maplibregl.Map({
      container: mapContainer.current,
      style: esriRasterStyle,
      center: [-8.2245, 39.3999],
      zoom: 5,
      minZoom: 3,
      maxZoom: 19,
      attributionControl: { compact: true },
    });

    instance.dragRotate.disable();
    instance.touchZoomRotate.disableRotation();
    instance.keyboard.disableRotation();

    map.current = instance;

    instance.addControl(
      new maplibregl.NavigationControl({ showCompass: false }),
      "top-right"
    );

    const resizeObserver = new ResizeObserver(() => instance.resize());
    if (mapContainer.current) resizeObserver.observe(mapContainer.current);
    const raf = requestAnimationFrame(() => instance.resize());

    return () => {
      cancelAnimationFrame(raf);
      resizeObserver.disconnect();
      instance.remove();
      map.current = null;
    };
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    const instance = map.current;
    if (!instance) return;

    const setupMarkers = () => {
      const existing = (instance as any)._hcMarkers as maplibregl.Marker[] | undefined;
      if (existing) existing.forEach((m) => m.remove());
      (instance as any)._hcMarkers = [];

      if (camposValidos.length === 0) return;

      const newMarkers: maplibregl.Marker[] = [];

      camposValidos.forEach((c) => {
        // IMPORTANTE: 'el' é o contentor gerido pelo MapLibre (tem de ser position: absolute; top: 0; left: 0;)
        const el = document.createElement("div");
        el.className = "hc-marker-root";
        
        // Z-Index dinâmico no elemento raiz para garantir que o hover fica por cima dos outros pinos
        el.addEventListener('mouseenter', () => { el.style.zIndex = "999"; });
        el.addEventListener('mouseleave', () => { el.style.zIndex = ""; });

        // O '.hc-marker-container' com position: relative vai DENTRO de 'el' para não estragar a posição geográfica!
        el.innerHTML = `
          <div class="hc-marker-container">
            <div class="hc-pin-svg">
              <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="36" height="36" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg">
                <path d="M12 2C8.13 2 5 5.13 5 9c0 5.25 7 13 7 13s7-7.75 7-13c0-3.87-3.13-7-7-7zm0 9.5c-1.38 0-2.5-1.12-2.5-2.5s1.12-2.5 2.5-2.5 2.5 1.12 2.5 2.5-1.12 2.5-2.5 2.5z" fill="#e2a41d" stroke="#ffffff" stroke-width="1.5"/>
              </svg>
            </div>

            <!-- Ponte invisível para o rato não perder o hover entre o pino e o card -->
            <div class="hc-hover-bridge"></div>

            <!-- O Card do Campo (Pop-up via CSS Hover) -->
            <div class="hc-hover-popup">
              <div class="hc-popup-img-wrap">
                <img src="${c.imagem || FALLBACK_IMG}" alt="${escapeHtml(c.nome)}" class="hc-popup-img" loading="lazy" onerror="this.src='${FALLBACK_IMG}'"/>
                ${c.categoria ? `<span class="hc-popup-badge">${escapeHtml(c.categoria)}</span>` : ""}
              </div>
              <div class="hc-popup-body">
                <h3 class="hc-popup-title">${escapeHtml(c.nome)}</h3>
                <div class="hc-popup-footer">
                  ${c.preco != null && c.preco > 0 ? `
                    <div>
                      <span class="hc-popup-from">${isEn ? 'From' : 'Desde'}</span>
                      <p class="hc-popup-price">${c.preco}€</p>
                    </div>
                  ` : '<div></div>'}
                  <a href="/${lang}/campo/${c.id}" class="hc-popup-cta">
                    ${isEn ? "View Dates" : "Ver Datas"}
                  </a>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        `;

        // offset: [0, 3] compensa a margem interna inferior do viewBox 24x24 do SVG para cravar o bico na coordenada
        const marker = new maplibregl.Marker({ element: el, anchor: "bottom", offset: [0, 3] })
          .setLngLat([c.longitude, c.latitude])
          .addTo(instance);

        newMarkers.push(marker);
      });

      (instance as any)._hcMarkers = newMarkers;

      if (camposValidos.length === 1) {
        instance.flyTo({ center: [camposValidos[0].longitude, camposValidos[0].latitude], zoom: 11, duration: 800 });
      } else {
        const bounds = new maplibregl.LngLatBounds();
        camposValidos.forEach((c) => bounds.extend([c.longitude, c.latitude]));
        instance.fitBounds(bounds, { padding: 80, duration: 800, maxZoom: 12 });
      }
    };

    setupMarkers();
    instance.once("load", setupMarkers);
  }, [camposValidos, lang, isEn]);

  return (
    <>
      <style>{`
        /* Garante que o marcador raiz do MapLibre NUNCA entra em fluxo normal de blocos */
        .maplibregl-marker.hc-marker-root {
          position: absolute !important;
          top: 0 !important;
          left: 0 !important;
          width: 36px;
          height: 36px;
          pointer-events: auto;
        }

        /* O Contentor Interno do Pino */
        .hc-marker-container { 
          position: relative; 
          width: 36px; height: 36px; 
          display: flex; justify-content: center; align-items: flex-end; 
          cursor: pointer;
        }

        /* O SVG do Pino em si */
        .hc-pin-svg {
          display: flex;
          transform-origin: bottom center;
          transition: transform 0.2s cubic-bezier(0.34, 1.56, 0.64, 1);
          filter: drop-shadow(0px 4px 6px rgba(226, 164, 29, 0.4));
        }

        .hc-marker-container:hover .hc-pin-svg {
          transform: scale(1.15) translateY(-2px);
        }

        /* Ponte invisível para o Hover não quebrar */
        .hc-hover-bridge {
          position: absolute;
          bottom: 30px;
          left: 50%;
          transform: translateX(-50%);
          width: 60px;
          height: 20px;
          background: transparent;
        }

        /* O Card (Popup) que aparece por cima */
        .hc-hover-popup {
          position: absolute;
          bottom: 44px; /* Fica logo acima do pino */
          left: 50%;
          transform: translateX(-50%) translateY(10px);
          width: 260px;
          background: white;
          border-radius: 16px;
          box-shadow: 0 10px 30px -5px rgba(0,0,0,0.2);
          opacity: 0;
          visibility: hidden;
          transition: all 0.2s cubic-bezier(0.4, 0, 0.2, 1);
          pointer-events: none; /* Para não bloquear cliques até estar visível */
          overflow: hidden;
          border: 1px solid #f1f5f9;
        }

        /* A Magia do Hover: Mostrar o Card */
        .hc-marker-container:hover .hc-hover-popup {
          opacity: 1;
          visibility: visible;
          transform: translateX(-50%) translateY(0);
          pointer-events: auto; /* Permite clicar no botão "Ver Datas" */
        }

        /* Design do Interior do Card */
        .hc-popup-img-wrap { position: relative; width: 100%; height: 140px; background: #f8fafc; }
        .hc-popup-img { width: 100%; height: 100%; object-fit: cover; display: block; margin: 0; }
        .hc-popup-badge {
          position: absolute; top: 12px; left: 12px;
          background: rgba(255,255,255,0.95); backdrop-filter: blur(4px);
          color: #0f172a; font-size: 9px; font-weight: 900;
          text-transform: uppercase; letter-spacing: 0.08em;
          padding: 6px 10px; border-radius: 8px; box-shadow: 0 2px 8px rgba(0,0,0,0.05);
        }
        
        .hc-popup-body { padding: 16px; display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 12px; }
        
        .hc-popup-title {
          margin: 0; font-size: 15px; font-weight: 900; color: #0f172a; line-height: 1.3;
          display: -webkit-box; -webkit-line-clamp: 2; -webkit-box-orient: vertical; overflow: hidden; text-align: left;
        }
        
        .hc-popup-footer {
          display: flex; justify-content: space-between; align-items: flex-end;
          margin-top: 4px; padding-top: 12px; border-top: 1px solid #f1f5f9;
        }

        .hc-popup-from {
          display: block; font-size: 9px; font-weight: 800; color: #94a3b8; 
          text-transform: uppercase; letter-spacing: 0.05em; margin-bottom: 2px;
        }

        .hc-popup-price { margin: 0; font-size: 20px; font-weight: 900; color: #0f172a; text-align: left; line-height: 1;}
        
        .hc-popup-cta {
          background: #0f172a; color: white;
          font-size: 11px; font-weight: 900; padding: 10px 16px;
          border-radius: 10px; text-decoration: none;
          text-transform: uppercase; letter-spacing: 0.05em; transition: background 0.15s ease;
        }
        
        .hc-popup-cta:hover { background: #e2a41d; }
      `}</style>
      <div ref={mapContainer} className="w-full h-full bg-[#f1f5f9]" style={{ minHeight: "400px" }} />
    </>
  );
}

function escapeHtml(str: string): string {
  if (!str) return "";
  return String(str)
    .replace(/&/g, "&amp;")
    .replace(/</g, "&lt;")
    .replace(/>/g, "&gt;")
    .replace(/"/g, "&quot;")
    .replace(/'/g, "&#039;");
}
</file>

<file path="app/[lang]/(public)/campo/[id]/page.tsx">
import { Metadata } from "next";
import { supabase } from "@/lib/supabase";
import Link from "next/link";
import { ReservaProvider, SeletorOpcoes, CaixaResumo } from "./CaixaReserva";
import BotaoFavorito from "../../components/BotaoFavorito";
import BotaoPartilha from "../../components/BotaoPartilha";
import { getDictionary } from "@/lib/getDictionary";
import FormContactoCampo from "../../components/FormContactoCampo";
import DescricaoExpansivel from "../../components/DescricaoExpansivel";
import { unstable_cache } from "next/cache";

export const dynamic = 'force-dynamic';

const getCampoCached = unstable_cache(
  async (id: string) => {
    const { data } = await supabase.from('campos').select('*').eq('id', id).single();
    return data;
  },
  ['cache-campo-detalhe'],
  { revalidate: 1 }
);

const getReviewsCached = unstable_cache(
  async (id: string) => {
    const { data } = await supabase.from("reviews").select("*").eq("campo_id", id).order('created_at', { ascending: false });
    return data || [];
  },
  ['cache-campo-reviews'],
  { revalidate: 1 }
);

const getRelacionadosCached = unstable_cache(
  async (id: string, organizadorId: string | null, categoria: string | null) => {
    let outrosDoParceiro: any[] = [];
    if (organizadorId) {
      const { data } = await supabase.from('campos').select('id, nome, nome_en, imagem, distrito').eq('organizador_id', organizadorId).neq('id', id).limit(3);
      outrosDoParceiro = data || [];
    }

    let semelhantes: any[] = [];
    if (categoria) {
      const { data } = await supabase.from('campos').select('id, nome, nome_en, imagem, distrito').eq('categoria', categoria).neq('id', id).limit(3);
      semelhantes = data || [];
    }

    return { outrosDoParceiro, semelhantes };
  },
  ['cache-campo-relacionados'],
  { revalidate: 86400 } 
);

export async function generateMetadata({ params }: { params: Promise<{ lang: string; id: string }> }): Promise<Metadata> {
  const { lang, id } = await params;
  const isEn = lang === 'en';
  const campo = await getCampoCached(id);

  if (!campo) return { title: 'Camp | HelloCamp' };

  const title = (isEn && campo.nome_en ? campo.nome_en : campo.nome) + ' | HelloCamp';
  const descRaw = isEn && campo.descricao_en ? campo.descricao_en : campo.descricao;
  const description = descRaw ? descRaw.substring(0, 155) + '...' : '';

  return {
    title, description,
    openGraph: { title, description, images: [{ url: campo.imagem || '/og-image.jpg', width: 1200, height: 630 }] }
  };
}

export default async function DetalhesDoCampo({ params }: { params: Promise<{ lang: string; id: string }>; }) {
  const { lang, id } = await params;

  const dict = await getDictionary(lang as "pt" | "en");
  const isEn = lang === 'en';

  const campo = await getCampoCached(id);
  const reviews = await getReviewsCached(id);

  if (!campo) {
    return (
      <div className="flex min-h-screen flex-col items-center justify-center bg-slate-50 p-6 text-center font-sans">
        <div className="w-24 h-24 bg-white rounded-full flex items-center justify-center shadow-sm border border-slate-200 mb-6 text-4xl">🕵️‍♂️</div>
        <h1 className="text-3xl font-black text-slate-900">{isEn ? 'Camp not found' : 'Campo não encontrado'}</h1>
        <Link href={`/${lang}/pesquisa`} className="mt-6 bg-[#0E3A68] text-white px-6 py-3 rounded-xl font-bold shadow-md hover:bg-[#0b2b4d] transition-colors">
          {dict.detalhe.voltar_pesquisa}
        </Link>
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (campo.id && campo.organizador_id) {
    supabase.rpc('registar_visualizacao_campo', {
      p_campo_id: campo.id,
      p_organizador_id: campo.organizador_id
    }).then();
  }

  let parceiroInfo = null;
  if (campo.organizador_id) {
    const { data: organizador } = await supabase.from("perfis").select("*").eq("id", campo.organizador_id).single();
    parceiroInfo = organizador;
  }

  const { outrosDoParceiro, semelhantes } = await getRelacionadosCached(campo.id, campo.organizador_id, campo.categoria);

  const nomeCampo = isEn && campo.nome_en ? campo.nome_en : campo.nome;
  const descCampo = isEn && campo.descricao_en ? campo.descricao_en : campo.descricao;
  const catCampo = isEn && campo.categoria_en ? campo.categoria_en : campo.categoria;
  const localCampo = isEn && campo.local_en ? campo.local_en : (isEn && campo.Distrito_en ? campo.Distrito_en : (campo.Distrito || campo.distrito || campo.local));
  
  const racioCampo = isEn && campo.racio_monitores_en ? campo.racio_monitores_en : campo.racio_monitores;
  const alimentacaoCampo = isEn && campo.alimentacao_en ? campo.alimentacao_en : campo.alimentacao;
  const alojamentoCampo = isEn && campo.alojamento_en ? campo.alojamento_en : campo.alojamento;
  const seguroCampo = isEn && campo.seguro_en ? campo.seguro_en : campo.seguro;
  const regrasCampo = isEn && campo.regras_termos_en ? campo.regras_termos_en : campo.regras_termos;

  const paisReal = campo.pais || "Portugal";
  const paisVisivel = isEn && campo.pais_en ? campo.pais_en : paisReal;

  const scoreAvaliacoes = campo.rating_score || 0;
  const totalAvaliacoes = campo.total_reviews || 0;

  const baseUrl = "https://www.hellocamp.pt";
  const campoUrlCompleto = `${baseUrl}/${lang}/campo/${campo.id}`;

  const calendario = campo.calendario_funcionamento || { data_inicio: "", data_fim: "", dias_semana: [] };
  const todayISO = new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0];
  const dataMaisCedo = calendario.data_inicio || campo.data_inicio || todayISO;

  let precoMinimo = campo.preco || 0;
  if (campo.pacotes && campo.pacotes.length > 0 && !precoMinimo) {
    const todosPrecos = campo.pacotes.flatMap((p: any) => p.variantes ? p.variantes.map((v: any) => v.preco) : []);
    if (todosPrecos.length > 0) precoMinimo = Math.min(...todosPrecos);
  }

  const eventSchema = {
    "@context": "https://schema.org",
    "@type": "Event",
    "name": nomeCampo,
    "description": descCampo ? descCampo.substring(0, 155) : '',
    "image": campo.imagem,
    "startDate": dataMaisCedo,
    ...(calendario.data_fim && { "endDate": calendario.data_fim }),
    "eventAttendanceMode": "https://schema.org/OfflineEventAttendanceMode",
    "eventStatus": "https://schema.org/EventScheduled",
    "location": {
      "@type": "Place",
      "name": localCampo,
      "address": { "@type": "PostalAddress", "addressLocality": localCampo, "addressCountry": paisReal }
    },
    "offers": {
      "@type": "Offer",
      "price": precoMinimo,
      "priceCurrency": "EUR",
      "availability": "https://schema.org/InStock",
      "validFrom": todayISO
    },
    ...(totalAvaliacoes > 0 && {
      "aggregateRating": {
        "@type": "AggregateRating",
        "ratingValue": scoreAvaliacoes,
        "reviewCount": totalAvaliacoes
      }
    }),
    "organizer": {
      "@type": "Organization",
      "name": parceiroInfo ? (parceiroInfo.empresa_nome || parceiroInfo.nome_empresa || parceiroInfo.nome_completo) : "HelloCamp Partner"
    }
  };

  const breadcrumbSchema = {
    "@context": "https://schema.org",
    "@type": "BreadcrumbList",
    "itemListElement": [
      { "@type": "ListItem", "position": 1, "name": isEn ? "Home" : "Início", "item": `${baseUrl}/${lang}` },
      { "@type": "ListItem", "position": 2, "name": paisVisivel, "item": `${baseUrl}/${lang}/pesquisa/pais/${encodeURIComponent(paisReal)}` },
      { "@type": "ListItem", "position": 3, "name": localCampo, "item": `${baseUrl}/${lang}/distrito/${encodeURIComponent(campo.Distrito || localCampo)}` },
      { "@type": "ListItem", "position": 4, "name": nomeCampo, "item": campoUrlCompleto }
    ]
  };

  const heroImgUrl = campo.imagem?.startsWith('http') ? campo.imagem : '/og-image.jpg';
  
  const nomeExibicaoParceiro = parceiroInfo 
    ? (parceiroInfo.empresa_nome || parceiroInfo.nome_empresa || parceiroInfo.nome_completo || 'Parceiro HelloCamp')
    : 'Parceiro HelloCamp';

  return (
    <main className="min-h-screen bg-slate-50 font-sans text-slate-900 pb-24">
      <script type="application/ld+json" dangerouslySetInnerHTML={{ __html: JSON.stringify(breadcrumbSchema) }} />
      <script type="application/ld+json" dangerouslySetInnerHTML={{ __html: JSON.stringify(eventSchema) }} />

      {/* HERO SECTION */}
      <div className="relative w-full h-[40vh] min-h-[350px] md:h-[500px] bg-slate-900 overflow-hidden">
        <div 
          className="absolute inset-0 w-full h-full bg-cover bg-center opacity-90"
          style={{ backgroundImage: `url('${heroImgUrl}')` }}
        />
        <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-gradient-to-b from-slate-900/60 via-transparent to-transparent"></div>

        <div className="absolute top-6 left-6 z-30 hidden sm:block">
          <nav className="flex items-center gap-2 text-[10px] font-bold text-white/80 bg-slate-900/50 backdrop-blur-sm px-4 py-2 rounded-full shadow-md border border-white/10 tracking-widest uppercase">
            <Link href={`/${lang}`} className="hover:text-white transition-colors">{isEn ? 'Home' : 'Início'}</Link>
            <span className="text-white/40">/</span>
            <Link href={`/${lang}/pesquisa/pais/${encodeURIComponent(paisReal)}`} className="hover:text-white transition-colors">{paisVisivel}</Link>
            <span className="text-white/40">/</span>
            <Link href={`/${lang}/distrito/${encodeURIComponent(campo.Distrito || localCampo)}`} className="hover:text-white transition-colors">{localCampo}</Link>
            <span className="text-white/40">/</span>
            <span className="text-white max-w-[120px] sm:max-w-[150px] truncate" title={nomeCampo}>{nomeCampo}</span>
          </nav>
        </div>

        <div className="absolute top-6 left-4 z-30 sm:hidden">
          <Link href={`/${lang}/distrito/${encodeURIComponent(campo.Distrito || localCampo)}`} className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-full bg-slate-900/50 backdrop-blur-sm px-4 py-2.5 text-xs font-bold text-white shadow-md border border-white/10 uppercase tracking-widest">
            &larr; {localCampo}
          </Link>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="max-w-[1100px] mx-auto pt-8 pb-10 px-4 md:px-6">

        <ReservaProvider campo={campo} lang={lang}>
          <div className="flex flex-col lg:flex-row gap-8 items-start w-full">

            {/* COLUNA ESQUERDA */}
            <div className="flex-1 w-full flex flex-col gap-6">

              {/* CABEÇALHO DO CAMPO */}
              <div className="bg-white p-6 md:p-8 rounded-[2rem] shadow-sm border border-slate-200 relative z-40">
                <div className="absolute top-6 right-6 z-50 flex items-center gap-2">
                  <BotaoPartilha url={campoUrlCompleto} titulo={nomeCampo} isEn={isEn} />
                  <BotaoFavorito campoId={campo.id} />
                </div>

                <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 mb-4 pr-20">
                  <span className="rounded-lg bg-[#e2a41d]/10 text-[#e2a41d] px-3 py-1.5 text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest">{catCampo}</span>
                  <span className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-500 flex items-center gap-1">
                    <svg className="w-3.5 h-3.5" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="3"><path d="M21 10c0 7-9 13-9 13s-9-6-9-13a9 9 0 0 1 18 0z"></path><circle cx="12" cy="10" r="3"></circle></svg>
                    {localCampo}
                  </span>
                </div>

                <h1 className="text-3xl md:text-5xl font-black text-slate-900 leading-tight mb-4 tracking-tight">{nomeCampo}</h1>

                <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                  <span className="text-slate-900 bg-amber-100 px-2 py-1 rounded-md text-sm font-black">
                    ★ {scoreAvaliacoes > 0 ? scoreAvaliacoes.toFixed(1) : (isEn ? 'New' : 'Novo')}
                  </span>
                  <span className="text-slate-500 text-xs font-bold">
                    ({totalAvaliacoes} {isEn ? 'reviews' : 'avaliações'})
                  </span>
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* DESCRIÇÃO */}
              <div className="bg-white p-6 md:p-8 rounded-[2rem] shadow-sm border border-slate-200 relative z-10">
                <h2 className="text-xl font-black text-slate-900 mb-4">{dict.detalhe.sobre_programa}</h2>
                <div className="text-sm">
                  <DescricaoExpansivel texto={descCampo} isEn={isEn} />
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* INFORMAÇÕES LOGÍSTICAS IMPORTANTES */}
              <div className="bg-white p-6 md:p-8 rounded-[2rem] shadow-sm border border-slate-200 relative z-10">
                <h2 className="text-xl font-black text-slate-900 mb-6">{dict.detalhe.informacoes_importantes}</h2>
                <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                  <div className="flex items-start sm:items-center gap-3 p-4 bg-slate-50 rounded-2xl border border-slate-100 flex-col sm:flex-row">
                    <span className="text-2xl">👥</span>
                    <div><h4 className="font-bold text-[10px] text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-widest mb-1">{dict.detalhe.racio_monitores}</h4><p className="text-sm font-black text-slate-800 m-0 leading-tight">{racioCampo || dict.detalhe.sob_consulta}</p></div>
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex items-start sm:items-center gap-3 p-4 bg-slate-50 rounded-2xl border border-slate-100 flex-col sm:flex-row">
                    <span className="text-2xl">🍎</span>
                    <div><h4 className="font-bold text-[10px] text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-widest mb-1">{dict.detalhe.alimentacao}</h4><p className="text-sm font-black text-slate-800 m-0 leading-tight">{alimentacaoCampo || dict.detalhe.sob_consulta}</p></div>
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex items-start sm:items-center gap-3 p-4 bg-slate-50 rounded-2xl border border-slate-100 flex-col sm:flex-row">
                    <span className="text-2xl">🏕️</span>
                    <div><h4 className="font-bold text-[10px] text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-widest mb-1">{dict.detalhe.alojamento}</h4><p className="text-sm font-black text-slate-800 m-0 leading-tight">{alojamentoCampo || dict.detalhe.nao_inclui_dormida}</p></div>
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex items-start sm:items-center gap-3 p-4 bg-slate-50 rounded-2xl border border-slate-100 flex-col sm:flex-row">
                    <span className="text-2xl">🛡️</span>
                    <div><h4 className="font-bold text-[10px] text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-widest mb-1">{dict.detalhe.seguros}</h4><p className="text-sm font-black text-slate-800 m-0 leading-tight">{seguroCampo || dict.detalhe.sob_consulta}</p></div>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* CROSS-LINKING DE AUTORIDADE: PERFIL DO ORGANIZADOR */}
              {campo.organizador_id && parceiroInfo && (
                <div className="bg-white p-6 md:p-8 rounded-[2rem] shadow-sm border border-slate-200 relative z-10 hover:border-[#0E3A68]/20 transition-colors">
                  <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-start sm:items-center justify-between gap-6">
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-5">
                      <div className="w-20 h-20 bg-slate-50 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center overflow-hidden border border-slate-200 shadow-inner flex-shrink-0 p-3">
                        {parceiroInfo.logotipo_url ? (
                          <img src={parceiroInfo.logotipo_url} alt={nomeExibicaoParceiro} className="w-full h-full object-contain" />
                        ) : (
                          <span className="text-slate-300 text-3xl">🏢</span>
                        )}
                      </div>
                      <div>
                        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-1">
                          <h3 className="text-xl font-black text-slate-900 m-0 leading-none">
                            {nomeExibicaoParceiro}
                          </h3>
                          {parceiroInfo.parceiro_verificado && (
                            <span className="bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-700 rounded-full p-1 shadow-sm" title={isEn ? "Verified Partner" : "Parceiro Verificado"}>
                              <svg className="w-3.5 h-3.5" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="3"><path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" d="M5 13l4 4L19 7" /></svg>
                            </span>
                          )}
                        </div>
                        <p className="text-[10px] font-bold text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-widest mt-2">
                          {isEn ? 'Official Organizer' : 'Organizador Oficial'}
                        </p>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                    
                    <Link 
                      href={`/${lang}/parceiro/${campo.organizador_id}`} 
                      className="w-full sm:w-auto text-center bg-slate-50 hover:bg-slate-100 text-slate-900 border border-slate-200 font-black text-[10px] uppercase tracking-widest px-6 py-4 rounded-xl transition-all shadow-sm hover:shadow-md"
                    >
                      {isEn ? 'View Profile & Camps' : 'Ver Perfil e Campos'}
                    </Link>
                  </div>
                </div>
              )}

              {/* NAVEGAÇÃO INTELIGENTE COM O MAPA INCORPORADO E BOTÕES NATIVOS */}
              <div className="bg-white p-6 md:p-8 rounded-[2rem] shadow-sm border border-slate-200 relative z-10">
                <h2 className="text-xl font-black text-slate-900 mb-2">{isEn ? 'Location & Directions' : 'Localização e Como Chegar'}</h2>
                <p className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-500 flex items-center gap-2 leading-relaxed mb-6">
                  <svg className="w-5 h-5 text-[#e2a41d] shrink-0" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="2.5"><path d="M21 10c0 7-9 13-9 13s-9-6-9-13a9 9 0 0 1 18 0z"></path><circle cx="12" cy="10" r="3"></circle></svg>
                  {localCampo}
                </p>

                {/* O Teu Iframe Gracioso em Tons de Cinza */}
                <div className="w-full h-64 md:h-80 bg-slate-100 rounded-2xl mb-6 overflow-hidden border border-slate-200 relative group shadow-inner">
                  <iframe 
                    width="100%" height="100%" frameBorder="0" scrolling="no" marginHeight={0} marginWidth={0} loading="lazy"
                    src={`https://maps.google.com/maps?q=${(campo.latitude && campo.longitude) ? `${campo.latitude},${campo.longitude}` : encodeURIComponent(localCampo)}&z=15&output=embed`}
                    className="w-full h-full border-0 outline-none grayscale-[30%] contrast-[1.1] opacity-90 group-hover:grayscale-0 group-hover:opacity-100 transition-all duration-500"
                  ></iframe>
                </div>

                <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row gap-3">
                  <a href={`https://maps.apple.com/?daddr=${(campo.latitude && campo.longitude) ? `${campo.latitude},${campo.longitude}` : encodeURIComponent(localCampo)}`} target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" className="flex-1 flex items-center justify-center gap-2 bg-slate-50 hover:bg-slate-100 text-slate-900 px-5 py-3.5 rounded-xl text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest transition-colors border border-slate-200 shadow-sm">Apple Maps</a>
                  <a href={`https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&destination=${(campo.latitude && campo.longitude) ? `${campo.latitude},${campo.longitude}` : encodeURIComponent(localCampo)}`} target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" className="flex-1 flex items-center justify-center gap-2 bg-[#F0FDF4] hover:bg-[#DCFCE7] text-[#15803D] px-5 py-3.5 rounded-xl text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest transition-colors border border-[#BBF7D0] shadow-sm">Google Maps</a>
                  <a href={`https://waze.com/ul?ll=${(campo.latitude && campo.longitude) ? `${campo.latitude},${campo.longitude}` : ''}&q=${encodeURIComponent(localCampo)}&navigate=yes`} target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" className="flex-1 flex items-center justify-center gap-2 bg-[#EEF2FF] hover:bg-[#E0E7FF] text-[#4338CA] px-5 py-3.5 rounded-xl text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest transition-colors border border-[#C7D2FE] shadow-sm">Waze</a>
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* MOTOR DE RESERVA INTERATIVO */}
              <SeletorOpcoes />

              {/* REGRAS E TERMOS ESPECÍFICOS */}
              {regrasCampo && (
                <div className="bg-white p-6 md:p-8 rounded-[2rem] shadow-sm border border-slate-200 relative z-10">
                  <h2 className="text-xl font-black text-slate-900 mb-4">{isEn ? 'Specific Rules & Terms' : 'Regras e Termos'}</h2>
                  <p className="leading-relaxed text-slate-600 text-sm whitespace-pre-wrap font-medium m-0 bg-slate-50 p-6 rounded-2xl border border-slate-100">{regrasCampo}</p>
                </div>
              )}

              {/* SECÇÃO DE AVALIAÇÕES */}
              <div className="bg-white p-6 md:p-8 rounded-[2rem] shadow-sm border border-slate-200 relative z-10">
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-6 border-b border-slate-100 pb-4">
                  <h2 className="text-xl font-black text-slate-900 m-0">{isEn ? 'Reviews' : 'Avaliações'}</h2>
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                    <span className="text-slate-900 bg-amber-100 px-2 py-1 rounded-md text-sm font-black">
                      ★ {scoreAvaliacoes > 0 ? scoreAvaliacoes.toFixed(1) : (isEn ? 'New' : 'Novo')}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                </div>

                {!reviews || reviews.length === 0 ? (
                  <div className="text-center py-6 text-slate-500 font-bold text-sm bg-slate-50 rounded-2xl border border-slate-100 border-dashed">
                    {isEn ? 'No reviews for this camp yet. Be the first!' : 'Ainda não há avaliações. Seja o primeiro a deixar opinião após o campo!'}
                  </div>
                ) : (
                  <div className="flex flex-col gap-6">
                    {reviews.map((rev: any) => (
                      <div key={rev.id} className="flex flex-col gap-2 pb-6 border-b border-slate-100 last:border-0 last:pb-0">
                        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                          <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-full bg-slate-200 flex items-center justify-center font-black text-slate-600 text-sm flex-shrink-0">
                            {rev.nome_pai ? rev.nome_pai.charAt(0).toUpperCase() : 'A'}
                          </div>
                          <div>
                            <p className="font-black text-sm text-slate-900 m-0">{rev.nome_pai || 'Anónimo'}</p>
                            <p className="text-[10px] font-bold text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-widest m-0">{new Date(rev.created_at).toLocaleDateString(isEn ? 'en-US' : 'pt-PT')}</p>
                          </div>
                        </div>
                        <div className="text-[#EBA914] text-xs">{'★'.repeat(rev.rating)}{'☆'.repeat(5 - rev.rating)}</div>
                        <p className="text-sm text-slate-600 m-0 font-medium leading-relaxed bg-slate-50 p-4 rounded-xl border border-slate-100">{rev.comentario}</p>
                      </div>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>

              {/* GALERIA */}
              {campo.galeria && Array.isArray(campo.galeria) && campo.galeria.length > 0 && (
                <div className="bg-white p-6 md:p-8 rounded-[2rem] shadow-sm border border-slate-200 relative z-10">
                  <h2 className="text-xl font-black text-slate-900 mb-6">{isEn ? 'Photo Gallery' : 'Mais Fotografias'}</h2>
                  <div className="grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
                    {campo.galeria.map((imgUrl: string, idx: number) => {
                      const gImg = imgUrl.startsWith('http') ? imgUrl : '/og-image.jpg';
                      return (
                        <div key={idx} className="aspect-square rounded-2xl overflow-hidden bg-slate-100 border border-slate-200 relative">
                          <div 
                            className="w-full h-full bg-cover bg-center hover:scale-110 transition-transform duration-500"
                            style={{ backgroundImage: `url('${gImg}')` }}
                          />
                        </div>
                      )
                    })}
                  </div>
                </div>
              )}

              {/* CROSS SELLING: OUTROS CAMPOS DO PARCEIRO */}
              {outrosDoParceiro.length > 0 && (
                <div className="bg-white p-6 md:p-8 rounded-[2rem] shadow-sm border border-slate-200 relative z-10">
                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-6">
                    <h2 className="text-xl font-black text-slate-900 m-0">{isEn ? `More from ${nomeExibicaoParceiro}` : `Mais de ${nomeExibicaoParceiro}`}</h2>
                    {campo.organizador_id && <Link href={`/${lang}/parceiro/${campo.organizador_id}`} className="text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-[#0E3A68] hover:underline">{isEn ? 'View all' : 'Ver todos'}</Link>}
                  </div>
                  <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
                    {outrosDoParceiro.map((c: any) => {
                      const cImg = c.imagem?.startsWith('http') ? c.imagem : '/og-image.jpg';
                      return (
                        <Link key={c.id} href={`/${lang}/campo/${c.id}`} className="group flex flex-col gap-2 rounded-2xl bg-slate-50 p-2 border border-slate-100 hover:border-[#0E3A68]/30 transition-colors">
                          <div className="w-full aspect-[4/3] rounded-xl overflow-hidden bg-slate-200 relative">
                            <div className="w-full h-full bg-cover bg-center group-hover:scale-105 transition-transform duration-500" style={{ backgroundImage: `url('${cImg}')` }} />
                          </div>
                          <div className="px-2 pb-2 mt-1">
                            <p className="text-[10px] font-bold text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-widest mb-1 truncate">{c.distrito}</p>
                            <h3 className="text-sm font-black text-slate-900 leading-tight line-clamp-2 group-hover:text-[#0E3A68] transition-colors">{isEn && c.nome_en ? c.nome_en : c.nome}</h3>
                          </div>
                        </Link>
                      )
                    })}
                  </div>
                </div>
              )}

              {/* CROSS SELLING: CAMPOS SEMELHANTES */}
              {semelhantes.length > 0 && (
                <div className="bg-white p-6 md:p-8 rounded-[2rem] shadow-sm border border-slate-200 relative z-10">
                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-6">
                    <h2 className="text-xl font-black text-slate-900 m-0">{isEn ? 'Similar Camps' : 'Campos Semelhantes'}</h2>
                    <Link href={`/${lang}/pesquisa?categoria=${encodeURIComponent(campo.categoria || '')}`} className="text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-[#0E3A68] hover:underline">{isEn ? 'View all' : 'Ver mais'}</Link>
                  </div>
                  <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
                    {semelhantes.map((c: any) => {
                      const cImg = c.imagem?.startsWith('http') ? c.imagem : '/og-image.jpg';
                      return (
                        <Link key={c.id} href={`/${lang}/campo/${c.id}`} className="group flex flex-col gap-2 rounded-2xl bg-slate-50 p-2 border border-slate-100 hover:border-[#0E3A68]/30 transition-colors">
                          <div className="w-full aspect-[4/3] rounded-xl overflow-hidden bg-slate-200 relative">
                            <div className="w-full h-full bg-cover bg-center group-hover:scale-105 transition-transform duration-500" style={{ backgroundImage: `url('${cImg}')` }} />
                          </div>
                          <div className="px-2 pb-2 mt-1">
                            <p className="text-[10px] font-bold text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-widest mb-1 truncate">{c.distrito}</p>
                            <h3 className="text-sm font-black text-slate-900 leading-tight line-clamp-2 group-hover:text-[#0E3A68] transition-colors">{isEn && c.nome_en ? c.nome_en : c.nome}</h3>
                          </div>
                        </Link>
                      )
                    })}
                  </div>
                </div>
              )}

              {/* FORMULÁRIO DE CONTACTO */}
              <div id="duvidas" className="bg-white p-6 md:p-8 rounded-[2rem] shadow-sm border border-slate-200 relative z-10 scroll-mt-24">
                <h3 className="text-xl font-black text-slate-900 mb-2">{dict.detalhe.duvidas_titulo}</h3>
                <p className="text-sm text-slate-500 font-medium mb-6">{dict.detalhe.duvidas_sub}</p>
                <FormContactoCampo campoId={campo.id} organizadorId={campo.organizador_id} nomeCampo={nomeCampo} dict={dict} isEn={isEn} lang={lang} />
              </div>

            </div>

            {/* BARRA LATERAL COM O RESUMO FIXO */}
            <div className="w-full lg:w-[380px] flex-shrink-0 lg:sticky lg:top-8 relative z-30">
              <CaixaResumo />
            </div>
          </div>
        </ReservaProvider>
      </div>
    </main>
  );
}
</file>

</files>
